Generation walkthrough

Episode 2 / generation walkthrough

The finished episode, followed by its source sections, exact generation recipes, continuation inputs and version history.

My finished export · Download native episode

All in-scope videos use Seedance 2.5 at 480p, as confirmed by the creator; no upscale mode was used. Exact provider identifiers remain in technical metadata. Original scripts, authoring revisions and submitted payloads are kept separate.

The office reference chain

The submitted image order is Malfoid, Harry / Male 1, hood-up Foideldore and the actual Office v2 reroll. Continuations prepend the recorded video tail and append the selected source frame. The tower exterior remains shared episode-design material; it is not silently added as an extra submitted input.

Section 1 / Shots 01–06

section01-v3-98c54278
Shots 01–06Seedance 2.5 · 480p

Completed alternative / isolated partial.

Download native video · Open exact prompt text

Exact saved submitted prompt.

Prompt text

Download exact text

[TITLE]
The Case for Malfoid

[REFERENCES]
@image1: Malfoid's identity and uniform; exclude the upright full-body wand-display pose and pale background.
@image2: Male 1's identity, uniform and cheek scrape; exclude the expression panels and front/side/back lineup.
@image3: Foideldore's costume; use the hood-up back silhouette only here. Do not show the front or profile views, face, hair, waist or feminine body contours.
@image4: Office architecture, furnishings and materials, not its empty symmetrical camera composition. Populate it using the shots below.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive face and full-body acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Urgent moral protest against an unmoving authority; cutting sidelong irritation. Weighted cloth, crisp expression changes and deliberate holds between gestures.

[DURATION]
26.5 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loosened burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
Personality: Leads with his chest and open hands; exhaustion roughens his conviction without weakening it.
State in this scene: Standing before the desk, arguing for Malfoid; she stands slightly behind and beside him.
Motion anchor: Addresses Foideldore throughout. Small forward step, distinct open-hand appeals; never poses for Malfoid or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, natural mid-range, slightly roughened by exhaustion; earnest and increasingly frustrated, not a continuous shout.

MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal V-neck sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud chin, hip-weighted stance, incisive eye movements; annoyance is openly readable.
State in this scene: Beside Male 1, initially watching Foideldore, then watching him.
Motion anchor: Folds her arms, side-eyes him, rolls her eyes; keeps her place. Inner dialogue has no lip movement.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register, precise consonants and a dry edge; thoughts sound private, not whispered aloud.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Heavy wine-red floor-length cloak, broad sleeves, navy hood lining; hood raised, body concealed by uninterrupted hanging folds.
State in this scene: Back to the exterior camera; the interior view catches the end of her turn toward the windows.
Motion anchor: Her heavy cloak trails the turn and settles as she takes her final step. Remains back-facing and silent afterwards.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's stone walls, dark wooden floor, heavy carved desk, curved rear bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Candles, books, parchment and brass instruments; elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, landscape right. Night rooftops beyond the glass.
Blocking: Windows behind the desk. From the visitor side, Male 1 stands left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind; Foideldore stands beyond the desk near the windows. Preserve these physical positions through angle changes.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–03.5 — SHOT 01:
Camera: Eye-level exterior wide through the tower windows; slow push toward the glass.
Action: Gothic tower above dark roofs and spires. Foideldore stands nearest the glass, back to camera, facing Male 1 deeper inside. His body pitches toward the argument, one hand raised; Malfoid stands slightly behind him.
Lighting: Blue moonlit stone; amber office light silhouettes the hood.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「本気で彼女をアズカバンに送るつもりなんですか？」
Delivery: Incredulous challenge, pressure rising toward the question's end.
VFX: Thin mist passes outside.
SFX: Wind; his voice slightly muffled through glass.

03.5–10.5 — SHOT 02:
Camera: Interior rear three-quarter medium-wide behind the students, across the desk; slow push-in.
Action: Foideldore’s hooded back is already toward us. Her heavy cloak swings sideways with the last of the turn, its hem trailing across the floor. The folds settle down her back as she takes the final step toward the windows. Male 1 follows with his eyes, then takes one small step toward the desk, opening his hand more insistently. Malfoid shifts onto one hip.
Lighting: Moonlight across the hood's cloth folds; warm hands and uniforms.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「校長先生、マルフォイドがいなかったら、僕はヴォルデモートに殺されてた。ロンも、ハーマイオニーも。」
Delivery: Direct appeal; the last two names land separately with personal weight.
SFX: Footsteps, candle crackle, window wind.

10.5–14.5 — SHOT 03:
Camera: Locked three-quarter medium close-up on Male 1, Malfoid farther behind his shoulder.
Action: His raised hand lowers, then opens again as if the fact should settle the matter. Brows draw together; he keeps addressing Foideldore offscreen. Behind him, Malfoid's eyes leave the windows and settle on his profile.
Lighting: Warm facial planes, blue edge along glasses and hair.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「僕たちがあそこから生きて帰れたのは、彼女のおかげです。」
Delivery: Emphatic gratitude, clearer and more personal than the preceding protest. His line continues over the cut into Shot 04.

14.5–17.0 — SHOT 04:
Camera: Locked tight three-quarter close-up on Malfoid; Male 1's shoulder blurred in foreground.
Action: She folds her arms with a decisive sleeve movement, tracks his gesturing hand, then cuts a visibly annoyed side-glance up toward his face. Her mouth stays closed and tight.
Lighting: Amber cheek light; moonlight softly shades her white hair.
Internal voiceover: MALFOID; 「ほんと、放っておけないのね。」
Delivery: Dry exasperation with a downward, dismissive finish.
Audio: Male 1's preceding line continues audibly beneath Malfoid's dry internal voiceover.

17.0–23.0 — SHOT 05:
Camera: Oblique lateral room view; very slow push toward Foideldore's back-facing silhouette.
Action: Foideldore occupies the window-side frame edge, hood hiding every facial angle; both students remain readable across the desk toward the opposite side. Male 1 presses his case. Malfoid rolls her eyes conspicuously and tilts her head away, arms firmly crossed, mouth closed. Foideldore gives no response.
Lighting: Moonlight across the robe's folds; warm students and desk.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「彼女がヴォルデモートを止めたんです。あそこにいた連中も、全部。」
Delivery: Insistent, stressing the scale of what Malfoid did.
Internal voiceover: MALFOID; 「いつも自分が助けなきゃ気が済まない。」
Delivery: Sharper private judgment; overlaps Male 1's ongoing line.
SFX: Candle crackle and distant wind.

23.0–26.5 — SHOT 06:
Camera: Malfoid's eyeline, slight low-angle three-quarter medium close-up on Male 1; slow push.
Action: Still looking toward Foideldore, he makes his last appeal. His final hand gesture settles at his side; jaw set, eyes completely sincere. Hold his unperformed conviction after the line.
Lighting: Blue-white catches his cheek and messy hair over amber fill.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「それで彼女を罰するなんて、おかしいでしょう。」
Delivery: Frustration peaks on the injustice, not on self-importance.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Male 1's exact Japanese lines form one continuous appeal across Shots 02–06. Malfoid's internal voiceover overlaps him, his speech audible underneath. Candle crackle, wind, footsteps and fabric. No music or subtitles.
Subtext: Male 1 is defending her because it is right; Malfoid finds his need to intervene infuriating. Do not reveal attraction early.
Critical constraint: All six shots in order. Foideldore's gender remains visually concealed throughout: hood up, no face/profile/hair, body outline or reflection. Exactly three characters; reference sheets never become scene layouts. No new injuries, props or spells.
Animation style: Japanese anime anticipation, accented brows and eyes, decisive poses, sleeve and hair follow-through. Foideldore's stillness contrasts with active student performances; do not flatten everyone into restraint.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicated characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition, live action and chibi deformation.

Reference inputs in order

Exact submitted order and hashes verified.

  1. @image1 · Malfoid · Open native image · design recipe
  2. @image2 · Male 1 · Open native image · design recipe
  3. @image3 · Foideldore · Open native image · design recipe
  4. @image4 · Office · Open native image · design recipe

The Case for Malfoid

Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register, precise consonants and a dry edge; her inner thoughts are heard close and clear.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Heavy wine-red floor-length cloak, broad sleeves, navy hood lining; hood raised, body concealed by uninterrupted hanging folds.
State in this scene: Initially nearest the glass, back to the exterior camera, facing the students. Begins turning toward the windows in Shot 01; the same turn finishes in Shot 02.
Motion anchor: One ordinary turn and a final step toward the windows; the lower cloak folds sway slightly and settle. Once facing outside, remains back-facing and silent.

TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's stone walls, dark wooden floor, heavy carved desk, curved rear bookcases and three pointed windows.

Blocking: Windows behind the desk. From the visitor side, Male 1 stands left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind; Foideldore stands beyond the desk near the windows. Preserve these physical positions through angle changes.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]

HARD CUT during the turn, just before her face or front comes into view.

Delivery: Incredulous challenge, pressure rising toward the question's end.
VFX: Thin mist passes outside.
SFX: Wind; his voice slightly muffled through glass.

03.5–10.5 — SHOT 02:
Camera: Interior rear three-quarter medium-wide behind the students, across the desk; slow push-in.

She finishes facing the windows; the cloak's lower folds sway slightly with her final step, then hang still. Male 1 follows with his eyes, then takes one small step toward the desk, opening his hand more insistently. Malfoid shifts onto one hip.
Lighting: Moonlight across the hood's cloth folds; warm hands and uniforms.

Delivery: Direct appeal; the last two names land separately with personal weight.
SFX: Footsteps, candle crackle, window wind.

10.5–14.5 — SHOT 03:
Camera: Locked three-quarter medium close-up on Male 1, Malfoid farther behind his shoulder.
Action: His raised hand lowers, then opens again as if the fact should settle the matter. Brows draw together; he keeps addressing Foideldore offscreen. Behind him, Malfoid's eyes leave the windows and settle on his profile.
Lighting: Warm facial planes, blue edge along glasses and hair.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「僕たちがあそこから生きて帰れたのは、彼女のおかげです。」
Delivery: Emphatic gratitude, clearer and more personal than the preceding protest.

14.5–23.0 — SHOTS 04–05 / HARRY'S SPEECH AND MALFOID'S THOUGHTS:
Camera: Hold Male 1's three-quarter medium close-up; internal cuts follow Malfoid's reactions and eyeline.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「彼女がヴォルデモートを止めたんです。あそこにいた連中も、全部。」
Delivery: Insistent, stressing the scale of what Malfoid did; one continuous passage across both cuts.
Action: Male 1 begins this line while the camera still holds him. At 15.5s, cut to the existing tight three-quarter frontal close-up on Malfoid: his shoulder is soft at frame-left, her face fills frame-right. She folds her arms, follows his gesturing hand with her eyes, and gives him a visibly annoyed side-glance. She thinks to herself with dry exasperation, 「ほんと、放っておけないのね。」 His speech continues behind her.
Still listening, she rolls her eyes and tilts her head slightly away. Her gaze returns to him as she thinks to herself more sharply, 「いつも自分が助けなきゃ気が済まない。」 At 19.0s, while her second thought and his line are both continuing, cut to a locked medium side-profile of Male 1 from Malfoid's eyeline. His open hand appeals toward Foideldore offscreen; his face and gesture fill the frame. Her thought finishes over this view of the behaviour she is judging, while he continues speaking.
Lighting: Amber facial light, soft moonlight shading hair and cloth.
SFX: His continuous speech becomes quieter and slightly muffled beneath her heard thoughts; candle crackle and distant wind.

23.0–26.5 — SHOT 06:
Camera: Malfoid's eyeline, slight low-angle three-quarter medium close-up on Male 1; slow push.
Action: Still looking toward Foideldore, he makes his last appeal. His final hand gesture settles at his side; jaw set, eyes completely sincere. Hold his unperformed conviction after the line.
Lighting: Blue-white catches his cheek and messy hair over amber fill.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「それで彼女を罰するなんて、おかしいでしょう。」
Delivery: Frustration peaks on the injustice, not on self-importance.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Male 1's exact Japanese appeal continues across the picture cuts. During Malfoid's inner thoughts, his ongoing voice drops in level and becomes slightly muffled in the mix beneath her close, clear, dry thoughts. His delivery stays earnest and forceful; his voice returns to normal prominence after her second thought. Candle crackle, wind, footsteps and fabric. No music or subtitles.
Subtext: Male 1 is defending her because it is right; Malfoid finds his need to intervene infuriating. Do not reveal attraction early.
Critical constraint: Foideldore's turn begins in Shot 01; HARD CUT before her face or front is revealed, then Shot 02 completes the same turn from behind. Her raised hood and heavy cloak conceal her face, front and gender throughout this clip. Preserve the source beat order and exact Japanese wording. Exactly three characters; reference sheets never become scene layouts. No new injuries, props or spells.

[PROMPT START]
Continue from @video1.

[TITLE]
Absolutely Not Potter

[REFERENCES]
@video1: Video continuation reference for character state, environment, spatial continuity, visual style and established voices.
@image1: Malfoid's identity and uniform; exclude the static full-body wand-display pose and pale backdrop.
@image2: Male 1's identity, uniform and cheek scrape; exclude the expression-panel collage and turnaround lineup.
@image3: Foideldore's hood-up back silhouette and cloak only; exclude every front/profile view and all readable feminine features.
@image4: Office architecture, furnishings and materials; do not reproduce the empty symmetrical composition.
@image5: Selected original-source continuation frame for character state, spatial continuity, lighting and visual style. The shot sequence defines the camera angles.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive character acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Irritation melts into attraction, snaps into mortification, then erupts as defensive hostility. Sharp reaction changes alternate with uncomfortable eye-contact holds.

[DURATION]
30 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud posture and precise diction; her eyes and shoulders betray feelings before she can recover her pose.
State in this scene: Standing right of Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded; watching his sincere defence of her.
Motion anchor: Arms loosen with attraction, clamp shut with embarrassment, open for confrontation, then fold again. No wand drawn. Inner voice never moves her lips.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register, dry precise articulation. Preserve voice identity while delivery swings from unguarded fascination to inward panic and biting irritation.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Left of Malfoid, slightly nearer the desk, finishing an appeal toward the hooded head of school.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. Never flirts deliberately, touches her or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Beyond the desk at the windows, back to the room, silent.
Motion anchor: No turn or visible face, hair, profile or gender cue. Frustration accumulates through stiffening shoulders and a small head drop.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Blocking: Male 1 left and slightly forward, Malfoid right on the visitor side; Foideldore beyond the desk facing the windows. Argument angles preserve their positions and eyelines without crossing the pair's axis.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–03.0 — SHOT 07:
Camera: Tight three-quarter close-up on Malfoid; slow push toward her face.
Action: Her eyes finally settle on Male 1. The irritated brow releases; her gaze lingers. Folded arms loosen and shoulders drop without permission. Lips part slightly; hold her absorbed stare long enough for the attraction to read.
Lighting: Warm face; blue window reflections catch in her eyes.
Dialogue: MALFOID, inner voice; 「嘘……かっこいい。」
Delivery: The first word escapes in disbelief; the admission is involuntarily breath-caught, without her usual defensive edge.
VFX: Office detail softens behind her; no hearts or fantasy cutaway.

03.0–06.5 — SHOT 08:
Camera: Locked slightly canted high-angle close-up on Malfoid, with exaggerated negative space.
Action: Eyes spring wide as she catches the thought. Flush spreads clearly across both cheeks. She snaps her gaze away and clamps her arms shut; eyes flick straight back toward him, then stop. Jaw tightens as mortification is forced into an angry mask.
Lighting: Cold rim flashes along the turning white hair; warm blush readable.
Dialogue: MALFOID, inner voice; 「ダメ。絶対にダメ。こいつはポッターよ。」
Delivery: Rapid private self-rebuke, each refusal more emphatic; his name becomes her defensive justification.
VFX: One brief graphic anime accent when she catches the returning glance; no chibi transformation.

06.5–13.0 — SHOT 09:
Camera: Locked wide side-profile two-shot across the near desk edge.
Action: Male 1 remains left, partly oriented toward Foideldore. Malfoid, right, pivots sharply toward him and steps into their shared space without crossing him. Arms unfold, chin lifts; the blush has not quite cleared as embarrassment bursts outward as hostility. Foideldore stays distant and back-facing between them.
Lighting: Warm students; distant blue-rimmed hood.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「誰にも庇ってもらう必要なんてないわ。特に、かの有名なハリー・ポッターにはね。」
Delivery: Cutting interruption; clipped first sentence, pointed sarcastic weight on his famous name. No coy smile.
SFX: Her abrupt step and robe movement.

13.0–17.5 — SHOT 10:
Camera: Locked medium over Malfoid's white-haired shoulder toward Male 1.
Action: His half-raised hand stops, hangs, then drops. Brows lift in genuine confusion. He turns fully toward her and opens his hand past her toward Foideldore, trying to return to what actually happened.
Lighting: Amber face; blue edge on black hair and glasses.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君の代わりに戦ってるんじゃない。何があったか話してるだけだ。」
Delivery: Baffled correction, growing friction; factual rather than patronizing.

17.5–20.0 — SHOT 11:
Camera: Locked tight side-profile two-shot, Male 1 left and Malfoid right.
Action: Malfoid leans closer instead of yielding, eyes narrowing. Male 1 holds her gaze, forehead creasing as he waits for an explanation. Let his short reply follow hers cleanly.
Lighting: Thin blue rims against the amber room.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「あなたって、いつもそう。」
Delivery: Accusatory familiarity, as though this offence is beyond obvious.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「何が？」
Delivery: Immediate, honestly bewildered.

20.0–23.0 — SHOT 12:
Camera: Locked rear medium close-up on Foideldore's hood and shoulders.
Action: Her shoulders become markedly rigid. The hood tips down a fraction while Malfoid continues accusing Male 1 offscreen. Foideldore remains completely back-facing; the heavy robe hides her body.
Lighting: Nearly black hood silhouette with a hard blue rim.
Dialogue: MALFOID, offscreen; 「何でも自分のことみたいに首を突っ込む。」
Delivery: Sharp and increasingly personal; not an inner thought.
SFX: Window wind beneath her voice.

23.0–26.5 — SHOT 13:
Camera: Locked tight three-quarter frontal close-up on Male 1 from Malfoid's side.
Action: He looks directly at her. The frustrated brow releases; hands lower completely and shoulders settle. He stops rebutting her and holds her eyes while stating the simplest possible truth. No flirtatious grin.
Lighting: Blue on one cheek; amber holds the other.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君に命を救われたんだ。だったら僕の問題だ。」
Delivery: Plain, firm and emotionally exposed; give the second sentence its own weight.

26.5–30.0 — SHOT 14:
Camera: Tight frontal close-up on Malfoid from his eyeline; slow push inward.
Action: She freezes. Anger drains; eyes widen and lips part without an answer. Hold the eye contact noticeably too long. Realization hits; her eyes snap aside before her head follows. She turns partly away, folds her arms and forces irritation back over the returning flush.
Lighting: Warm face through the hold; stronger blue hair rim as she turns.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「もう……ほんっと、イライラする。」
Delivery: Flustered breath into the opening; stress the complaint as she rebuilds her pride. The irritation is real but no longer conceals everything.
VFX: Held anime reaction followed by a sharply accented gaze break.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Exact Japanese lines, no additions or paraphrases. Inner voice is dry and close, without lip sync; spoken dialogue uses natural office acoustics. No invented murmuring, music or subtitles. Cuts follow complete lines.
Subtext: Attraction destabilizes Malfoid's pride; she attacks because she cannot tolerate how he makes her feel. Male 1 means his gratitude literally. Foideldore's motionless back accumulates pressure while their acting grows more volatile.
Critical constraint: Preserve all eight shots and their emotional order. Never reveal Foideldore's gender, face, profile, hair, body contours or reflection. Preserve reference identities without display poses, and the students' left/right relationship. No kiss, contact, new dialogue or added story beat.
Animation style: Full-range Japanese anime acting: eye darts, blush, sharp chin lifts, leaning silhouettes and delayed hair/sleeve follow-through. Strong poses and held reactions, not continuous low-intensity microexpression acting.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

[PROMPT START]
Continue from @video1.

[TITLE]
Absolutely Not Potter

[REFERENCES]
@video1: Video continuation reference for character state, environment, spatial continuity, visual style and established voices.
@image1: Malfoid's identity and uniform; exclude the static full-body wand-display pose and pale backdrop.
@image2: Male 1's identity, uniform and cheek scrape; exclude the expression-panel collage and turnaround lineup.
@image3: Foideldore's hood-up back silhouette and cloak only; exclude every front/profile view and all readable feminine features.
@image4: Office architecture, furnishings and materials; do not reproduce the empty symmetrical composition.
@image5: Selected original-source continuation frame for character state, spatial continuity, lighting and visual style. The shot sequence defines the camera angles.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive character acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Irritation melts into attraction, snaps into mortification, then erupts as defensive hostility. Sharp reaction changes alternate with uncomfortable eye-contact holds.

[DURATION]
30 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud posture and precise diction; her eyes and shoulders betray feelings before she can recover her pose.
State in this scene: Standing right of Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded; watching his sincere defence of her.
Motion anchor: Arms loosen with attraction, clamp shut with embarrassment, open for confrontation, then fold again. No wand drawn. Inner voice never moves her lips.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register, dry precise articulation. Preserve voice identity while delivery swings from unguarded fascination to inward panic and biting irritation.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Left of Malfoid, slightly nearer the desk, finishing an appeal toward the hooded head of school.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. Never flirts deliberately, touches her or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Beyond the desk at the windows, back to the room, silent.
Motion anchor: No turn or visible face, hair, profile or gender cue. Frustration accumulates through stiffening shoulders and a small head drop.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Blocking: Male 1 left and slightly forward, Malfoid right on the visitor side; Foideldore beyond the desk facing the windows. Argument angles preserve their positions and eyelines without crossing the pair's axis.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–03.0 — SHOT 07:
Camera: Tight three-quarter close-up on Malfoid; slow push toward her face.
Action: Her eyes finally settle on Male 1. The irritated brow releases; her gaze lingers. Folded arms loosen and shoulders drop without permission. Lips part slightly; hold her absorbed stare long enough for the attraction to read.
Lighting: Warm face; blue window reflections catch in her eyes.
Dialogue: MALFOID, inner voice; 「嘘……かっこいい。」
Delivery: The first word escapes in disbelief; the admission is involuntarily breath-caught, without her usual defensive edge.
VFX: Office detail softens behind her; no hearts or fantasy cutaway.

03.0–06.5 — SHOT 08:
Camera: Locked slightly canted high-angle close-up on Malfoid, with exaggerated negative space.
Action: Eyes spring wide as she catches the thought. Flush spreads clearly across both cheeks. She snaps her gaze away and clamps her arms shut; eyes flick straight back toward him, then stop. Jaw tightens as mortification is forced into an angry mask.
Lighting: Cold rim flashes along the turning white hair; warm blush readable.
Dialogue: MALFOID, inner voice; 「ダメ。絶対にダメ。こいつはポッターよ。」
Delivery: Rapid private self-rebuke, each refusal more emphatic; his name becomes her defensive justification.
VFX: One brief graphic anime accent when she catches the returning glance; no chibi transformation.

06.5–13.0 — SHOT 09:
Camera: Locked wide side-profile two-shot across the near desk edge.
Action: Male 1 remains left, partly oriented toward Foideldore. Malfoid, right, pivots sharply toward him and steps into their shared space without crossing him. Arms unfold, chin lifts; the blush has not quite cleared as embarrassment bursts outward as hostility. Foideldore stays distant and back-facing between them.
Lighting: Warm students; distant blue-rimmed hood.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「誰にも庇ってもらう必要なんてないわ。特に、かの有名なハリー・ポッターにはね。」
Delivery: Cutting interruption; clipped first sentence, pointed sarcastic weight on his famous name. No coy smile.
SFX: Her abrupt step and robe movement.

13.0–17.5 — SHOT 10:
Camera: Locked medium over Malfoid's white-haired shoulder toward Male 1.
Action: His half-raised hand stops, hangs, then drops. Brows lift in genuine confusion. He turns fully toward her and opens his hand past her toward Foideldore, trying to return to what actually happened.
Lighting: Amber face; blue edge on black hair and glasses.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君の代わりに戦ってるんじゃない。何があったか話してるだけだ。」
Delivery: Baffled correction, growing friction; factual rather than patronizing.

17.5–20.0 — SHOT 11:
Camera: Locked tight side-profile two-shot, Male 1 left and Malfoid right.
Action: Malfoid leans closer instead of yielding, eyes narrowing. Male 1 holds her gaze, forehead creasing as he waits for an explanation. Let his short reply follow hers cleanly.
Lighting: Thin blue rims against the amber room.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「あなたって、いつもそう。」
Delivery: Accusatory familiarity, as though this offence is beyond obvious.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「何が？」
Delivery: Immediate, honestly bewildered.

20.0–23.0 — SHOT 12:
Camera: Locked rear medium close-up on Foideldore's hood and shoulders.
Action: Her shoulders become markedly rigid. The hood tips down a fraction while Malfoid continues accusing Male 1 offscreen. Foideldore remains completely back-facing; the heavy robe hides her body.
Lighting: Nearly black hood silhouette with a hard blue rim.
Dialogue: MALFOID, offscreen; 「何でも自分のことみたいに首を突っ込む。」
Delivery: Sharp and increasingly personal; not an inner thought.
SFX: Window wind beneath her voice.

23.0–26.5 — SHOT 13:
Camera: Locked tight three-quarter frontal close-up on Male 1 from Malfoid's side.
Action: He looks directly at her. The frustrated brow releases; hands lower completely and shoulders settle. He stops rebutting her and holds her eyes while stating the simplest possible truth. No flirtatious grin.
Lighting: Blue on one cheek; amber holds the other.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君に命を救われたんだ。だったら僕の問題だ。」
Delivery: Plain, firm and emotionally exposed; give the second sentence its own weight.

26.5–30.0 — SHOT 14:
Camera: Tight frontal close-up on Malfoid from his eyeline; slow push inward.
Action: She freezes. Anger drains; eyes widen and lips part without an answer. Hold the eye contact noticeably too long. Realization hits; her eyes snap aside before her head follows. She turns partly away, folds her arms and forces irritation back over the returning flush.
Lighting: Warm face through the hold; stronger blue hair rim as she turns.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「もう……ほんっと、イライラする。」
Delivery: Flustered breath into the opening; stress the complaint as she rebuilds her pride. The irritation is real but no longer conceals everything.
VFX: Held anime reaction followed by a sharply accented gaze break.

Delivery: Accusatory familiarity, as though this offence is beyond obvious.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「何が？」
Delivery: Immediate, honestly bewildered.

20.0–23.0 — SHOT 12:
Camera: Locked rear medium close-up on Foideldore's hood and shoulders.
Action: Her shoulders become markedly rigid. The hood tips down a fraction while Malfoid continues accusing Male 1 offscreen. Foideldore remains completely back-facing; the heavy robe hides her body.
Lighting: Nearly black hood silhouette with a hard blue rim.
Dialogue: MALFOID, offscreen; 「何でも自分のことみたいに首を突っ込む。」
Delivery: Sharp and increasingly personal; not an inner thought.
SFX: Window wind beneath her voice.

23.0–26.5 — SHOT 13:
Camera: Locked tight three-quarter frontal close-up on Male 1 from Malfoid's side.
Action: He looks directly at her. The frustrated brow releases; hands lower completely and shoulders settle. He stops rebutting her and holds her eyes while stating the simplest possible truth. No flirtatious grin.
Lighting: Blue on one cheek; amber holds the other.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君に命を救われたんだ。だったら僕の問題だ。」
Delivery: Plain, firm and emotionally exposed; give the second sentence its own weight.

26.5–30.0 — SHOT 14:
Camera: Tight frontal close-up on Malfoid from his eyeline; slow push inward.
Action: Malfoid holds Male 1’s gaze for a beat too long, her attention lingering on his face again. Her blush deepens and her brows pull into an exasperated frown. Her eyes snap aside before her head follows. She turns partly away, folds her arms and delivers her complaint with pointed irritation, her cheeks still red.
Lighting: Warm face through the hold; stronger blue hair rim as she turns.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「もう……ほんっと、イライラする。」
Delivery: Flustered exasperation with pointed stress on the complaint; frustration at her own feelings keeps her tone irritated.
VFX: Held, flushed and visibly irritated eye contact followed by a sharply accented gaze break.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Exact Japanese lines, no additions or paraphrases. Inner voice is dry and close, without lip sync; spoken dialogue uses natural office acoustics. No invented murmuring, music or subtitles. Cuts follow complete lines.
Subtext: Malfoid finds Male 1 physically handsome while her attention wanders during his rant. Her own affectionate thought causes her blush. Frustration at her inability to control those feelings comes out as irritation toward him. Male 1 means his gratitude literally. Foideldore’s motionless back accumulates pressure while their argument grows more volatile.
Critical constraint: Preserve all eight shots and their emotional order. Never reveal Foideldore's gender, face, profile, hair, body contours or reflection. Preserve reference identities without display poses, and the students' left/right relationship. No kiss, contact, new dialogue or added story beat.
Animation style: Full-range Japanese anime acting: eye darts, blush, sharp chin lifts, leaning silhouettes and delayed hair/sleeve follow-through. Strong poses and held reactions, not continuous low-intensity microexpression acting.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive character acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Malfoid’s private attraction shows through a blush while she struggles to maintain her irritated, defensive attitude. Frustration at her own feelings sharpens into hostility toward Male 1. Lingering looks give way to a deepening blush, a frustrated frown and sharp, irritated side-glances.

[DURATION]
30 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud posture and precise diction; her eyes and shoulders betray feelings before she can recover her pose.
State in this scene: Standing right of Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded. During his rant, her attention wanders to his handsome face; her private thought makes her blush.
Motion anchor: Arms loosen slightly as her gaze lingers, clamp shut as she suppresses her feelings, open for confrontation, then fold again. Her silent reactions use a lingering gaze, visible blush, a frustrated frown and sharp gaze breaks. No wand drawn. Inner voice never moves her lips; spoken lines retain expressive articulation.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register, dry precise articulation. Preserve voice identity through private attraction, self-directed exasperation and biting irritation.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Left of Malfoid, slightly nearer the desk, finishing an appeal toward the hooded head of school.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. Never flirts deliberately, touches her or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Beyond the desk at the windows, back to the room, silent.
Motion anchor: No turn or visible face, hair, profile or gender cue. Frustration accumulates through stiffening shoulders and a small head drop.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:

Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Blocking: Male 1 left and slightly forward, Malfoid right on the visitor side; Foideldore beyond the desk facing the windows. Argument angles preserve their positions and eyelines without crossing the pair's axis.

[REFERENCES]
@video1: Video continuation reference for character state, environment, spatial continuity, visual style and established voices.

[AUDIO]
The first two Japanese lines are heard as Malfoid’s intimate internal voiceover. The handsome thought is dreamy and sincerely affectionate; the refusal is emphatic self-rebuke after the headshake. Her face and body perform the emotions while the audience hears her private thoughts. Her spoken confrontation begins in the next timed range with visible articulation and natural office acoustics. Preserve the established voice identities from @video1 and every exact Japanese line. Cuts follow completed lines.

[VISUAL STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive character acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

Malfoid’s annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes, an affectionate smile and pink cheeks. Warm pink haze and small rose-pink hearts accompany the daydream. A rapid headshake and sharp snap-zoom out restore the office and her defensive annoyance before the exaggerated spoken outburst.

[DURATION]
30 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud posture and precise diction; her eyes and shoulders betray feelings before she can recover her pose.
State in this scene: Standing right of Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded, watching him rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome brings a complete change into dreamy affection and a visible blush.
Motion anchor: Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile; she blushes and raises her hands beneath her chin. After the affectionate thought, a rapid side-to-side headshake breaks the daydream. She crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1 during her internal refusal, then unfolds one arm to point at him and shouts with exaggerated indignation, still blushing. Facial acting carries the thoughts; visible speech articulation begins with her spoken confrontation.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register and precise articulation. The handsome thought is soft, dreamy and sincerely affectionate internal voiceover. The internal refusal becomes brisk and emphatic after the headshake. Her first spoken interruption is a sharp, exaggerated shout with biting emphasis on his famous name; later accusations remain dry and pointed. Voice identity remains consistent through these changes.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Left of Malfoid, slightly nearer the desk, finishing an appeal toward the hooded head of school.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. His correction is baffled and factual; his gratitude is direct and sincere.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Beyond the desk at the windows, back to the room, silent.

00.0–06.5 seconds:
Starting from @image5, cut to a three-quarter frontal medium close-up of Malfoid watching Male 1. The camera slowly pushes toward her face. Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile. Her cheeks blush as she raises her hands beneath her chin and thinks to herself in a dreamy, affectionate inner voice, 「嘘……かっこいい。」 Simultaneously, the office background gradually blurs into a warm pink haze filled with small, translucent rose-pink hearts, softly glowing and drifting upward behind her. The push-in ends on her lovestruck face as the thought finishes.

She suddenly shakes her head rapidly from side to side. As she finishes the shake, the camera snap-zooms out to a medium shot and the romantic background instantly returns to the clearly defined office, restoring its amber light and blue window rim. Her expression immediately sharpens back into an annoyed frown, her cheeks still flushed. She crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1, thinking to herself in an emphatic, self-rebuking inner voice, 「ダメ。絶対にダメ。こいつはポッターよ。」

06.5–13.0 seconds:
Cut to a locked wide side-profile two-shot across the near desk edge, Male 1 on frame-left and Malfoid on frame-right. Foideldore remains back-facing in the background beyond the desk. Malfoid sharply turns toward Male 1 and steps forward on her side. She unfolds her arms and throws one hand out to point at him, leaning into the accusation with an exaggeratedly indignant expression, her cheeks still flushed. She shouts, {誰にも庇ってもらう必要なんてないわ。特に、かの有名なハリー・ポッターにはね。} The first sentence bursts out; his famous name receives pointed, biting emphasis. Warm light holds the students; a distant blue rim outlines Foideldore’s hood. <Abrupt step, robe and sleeve movement.>

13.0–17.5 seconds:
Cut to a locked medium OTS behind Malfoid’s white-haired shoulder, framing Male 1. His half-raised hand stops, hangs, then drops. His brows lift in genuine confusion. He turns fully toward her and opens his hand past her toward Foideldore, replying with baffled correction, growing friction and factual emphasis, {君の代わりに戦ってるんじゃない。何があったか話してるだけだ。} Amber light shapes his face; blue light edges his black hair and glasses.

17.5–20.0 seconds:
Cut to a locked tight side-profile two-shot, Male 1 left and Malfoid right. Malfoid leans closer, eyes narrowing, and says with accusatory familiarity, {あなたって、いつもそう。} Male 1 holds her gaze, his forehead creasing, then answers with immediate, honest bewilderment, {何が？} His short reply follows her completed line. Thin blue rims separate their profiles from the amber room.

20.0–23.0 seconds:
Cut to a locked rear medium close-up of Foideldore’s hood and shoulders. Her shoulders become markedly rigid and the hood tips down a fraction as Malfoid continues from offscreen with a sharp, increasingly personal spoken accusation, {何でも自分のことみたいに首を突っ込む。} Foideldore keeps her back toward the room, her heavy robe concealing her body. The nearly black hood has a hard blue rim. <Window wind beneath Malfoid’s voice.>

23.0–26.5 seconds:
Cut to a locked tight three-quarter frontal close-up on Male 1 from Malfoid’s side. He looks directly at her. His frustrated brow releases; his hands lower completely and his shoulders settle. Holding her eyes, he states plainly and firmly, emotionally exposed, {君に命を救われたんだ。だったら僕の問題だ。} The second sentence receives its own weight. Blue light catches one cheek; amber holds the other.

26.5–30.0 seconds:
Cut to a tight frontal close-up on Malfoid from Male 1’s eyeline; slow push inward. She holds his gaze for a beat too long, her attention lingering on his face again. Her blush deepens and her brows pull into an exasperated frown. Her eyes snap aside before her head follows. She turns partly away, folds her arms and complains with flustered exasperation and pointed irritation, {もう……ほんっと、イライラする。} Her cheeks remain red. Warm light holds her face through the eye contact; the blue hair rim strengthens as she turns. The gaze break receives a sharp graphic accent.

[FINAL VISUAL STYLE]
Full-range Japanese cel-anime acting with distinct emotional states and readable poses. A slow push into lovestruck affection is interrupted by the rapid headshake and a sharp snap-zoom out to a medium shot. Warm pink haze and small, translucent rose-pink hearts resolve back into the Gothic office during that reversal. The spoken outburst uses an exaggeratedly indignant expression, pointing gesture and visible blush. Preserve the reference identities, wardrobe, lighting geography and spatial continuity through the remaining exchange.

Subtext: Malfoid watches Male 1 rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome draws her into genuine, dreamy affection and makes her blush. She interrupts that state with a rapid headshake, thinks her refusal, recovers her defensive annoyance and snaps at him. Male 1 means his gratitude literally. Foideldore’s motionless back accumulates pressure while their argument grows more volatile.
Critical constraint: @image5 is the start frame. Preserve the chronological action, all exact Japanese wording and the 30-second duration. The first range combines source beats 07–08; the outburst occupies 06.5–13.0, and all later boundaries are retained. Both thought lines remain internal voiceover, with no lip synchronization to their words. The headshake follows the affectionate thought, the snap-zoom out follows the headshake, and the private refusal precedes the spoken snap. Foideldore remains back-facing and concealed throughout: no gender, face, profile, hair, body-contour or reflection reveal. Preserve reference identities without display poses and the students’ left/right relationship. No drawn wands, deliberate flirting by Male 1, kiss, physical contact, added dialogue, invented murmuring, music, subtitles or added story beat.

Avoid: Jitter, bent or distorted limbs. Temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register and precise articulation. The handsome thought is soft, dreamy and sincerely affectionate internal voiceover. The internal refusal becomes brisk and emphatic after the headshake. Her first spoken interruption is a sharp, exaggerated shout with biting emphasis on his famous name; later accusations remain dry and pointed. Voice identity remains consistent through these changes.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Left of Malfoid, slightly nearer the desk, finishing an appeal toward the hooded head of school.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. His correction is baffled and factual; his gratitude is direct and sincere.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Beyond the desk at the windows, back to the room, silent.
Motion anchor: Remains back-facing at the windows. Frustration accumulates through stiffening shoulders and a small head drop.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Blocking: Windows behind the desk. From the visitor side, Male 1 stands left and slightly forward, nearer the desk; Malfoid stands right and slightly behind him. Both students remain on the visitor side. Foideldore stands beyond the desk near the windows with her back toward the students. Their feet retain these positions while they turn, lean and gesture toward one another. Argument angles preserve their positions and eyelines.

00.0–06.5 seconds:
Starting from @image5, cut to a three-quarter frontal medium close-up of Malfoid watching Male 1. The camera slowly pushes toward her face. Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile. Her cheeks blush as she raises her hands beneath her chin and thinks to herself in a dreamy, affectionate inner voice, 「嘘……かっこいい。」 Simultaneously, the office background gradually blurs into a warm pink haze filled with small, translucent rose-pink hearts, softly glowing and drifting upward behind her. The push-in ends on her lovestruck face as the thought finishes.

She suddenly shakes her head rapidly from side to side. As she finishes the shake, the camera snap-zooms out to a medium shot and the romantic background instantly returns to the clearly defined office, restoring its amber light and blue window rim. Her expression immediately sharpens back into an annoyed frown, her cheeks still flushed. She crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1, thinking to herself in an emphatic, self-rebuking inner voice, 「ダメ。絶対にダメ。こいつはポッターよ。」

06.5–13.0 seconds:
Cut to a locked wide side-profile two-shot across the near desk edge. Male 1 stands frame-left and slightly forward, nearer the desk; Malfoid stands frame-right and slightly behind him, both on the visitor side. Foideldore remains back-facing near the windows on the opposite side of the desk. Malfoid holds her annoyed frown and crossed-arm pose, her cheeks flushed. From her position, she sharply turns toward Male 1, unfolds her arms and throws one hand out to point at him, leaning into the accusation with an exaggeratedly indignant expression and flushed cheeks. She shouts, {誰にも庇ってもらう必要なんてないわ。特に、かの有名なハリー・ポッターにはね。} The first sentence bursts out; his famous name receives pointed, biting emphasis. Warm light holds the students; a distant blue rim outlines Foideldore’s hood. <Robe and sleeve movement.>

13.0–17.5 seconds:
Cut to a locked medium OTS behind Malfoid’s white-haired shoulder, framing Male 1. Her pointing hand lowers as he begins his reply, leaving her arms unfolded. His half-raised hand stops, hangs, then drops. His brows lift in genuine confusion. He turns fully toward her and opens his hand past her toward Foideldore, replying with baffled correction, growing friction and factual emphasis, {君の代わりに戦ってるんじゃない。何があったか話してるだけだ。} Amber light shapes his face; blue light edges his black hair and glasses.

17.5–20.0 seconds:
Cut to a locked tight side-profile two-shot, Male 1 left and Malfoid right. Malfoid leans closer with her arms unfolded and cheeks flushed, her eyes narrowing as she says with accusatory familiarity, {あなたって、いつもそう。} Male 1 holds her gaze, his forehead creasing, then answers with immediate, honest bewilderment, {何が？} His short reply follows her completed line. Thin blue rims separate their profiles from the amber room.

20.0–23.0 seconds:
Cut to a locked rear medium close-up of Foideldore’s hood and shoulders. Her shoulders become markedly rigid and the hood tips down a fraction as Malfoid continues from offscreen with a sharp, increasingly personal spoken accusation, {何でも自分のことみたいに首を突っ込む。} Foideldore keeps her back toward the room, her heavy robe concealing her body. The nearly black hood has a hard blue rim. <Window wind beneath Malfoid’s voice.>

23.0–26.5 seconds:
Cut to a locked tight three-quarter frontal close-up on Male 1 from Malfoid’s side. He looks directly at her. His frustrated brow releases; his hands lower completely and his shoulders settle. Holding her eyes, he states plainly and firmly, emotionally exposed, {君に命を救われたんだ。だったら僕の問題だ。} The second sentence receives its own weight. Blue light catches one cheek; amber holds the other.

26.5–30.0 seconds:
Cut to a tight frontal close-up on Malfoid from Male 1’s eyeline; slow push inward. Malfoid holds his gaze for a beat too long, her flushed cheeks turning a deeper red. Her annoyed frown tightens as her eyes linger on his face. Her eyes snap aside before her head follows. She turns partly away, folds her arms and complains with flustered exasperation and pointed irritation, {もう……ほんっと、イライラする。} Her cheeks remain red. Warm light holds her face through the eye contact; the blue hair rim strengthens as she turns. The gaze break receives a sharp graphic accent.

[FINAL VISUAL STYLE]
Full-range Japanese cel-anime acting with distinct emotional states and readable poses. A slow push into lovestruck affection is interrupted by the rapid headshake and a sharp snap-zoom out to a medium shot. Warm pink haze and small, translucent rose-pink hearts resolve back into the Gothic office during that reversal. The spoken outburst uses an exaggeratedly indignant expression, pointing gesture and visible blush. Preserve the reference identities, wardrobe, lighting geography and spatial continuity through the remaining exchange.

Subtext: Malfoid watches Male 1 rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome draws her into genuine, dreamy affection and makes her blush. She interrupts that state with a rapid headshake, thinks her refusal, recovers her defensive annoyance and snaps at him. Male 1 means his gratitude literally. Foideldore’s motionless back accumulates pressure while their argument grows more volatile.
Critical constraint: @image5 is the start frame. Preserve the chronological action, all exact Japanese wording and the 30-second duration. The first range combines source beats 07–08; the outburst occupies 06.5–13.0, and all later boundaries are retained. Both thought lines remain internal voiceover, with no lip synchronization to their words. The headshake follows the affectionate thought, the snap-zoom out follows the headshake, and the private refusal precedes the spoken snap. Foideldore remains back-facing and concealed throughout: no gender, face, profile, hair, body-contour or reflection reveal. Preserve reference identities without display poses and the students’ left/right and depth relationship: Male 1 left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind, both on the visitor side. Carry the resulting character state through subsequent cuts until a described action changes it. No drawn wands, deliberate flirting by Male 1, kiss, physical contact, added dialogue, invented murmuring, music, subtitles or added story beat.

Avoid: Jitter, bent or distorted limbs. Temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

Preserve reference identities without display poses and the students’ left/right and depth relationship: Male 1 left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind, both on the visitor side. Carry the resulting character state through subsequent cuts until a described action changes it. No drawn wands, deliberate flirting by Male 1, kiss, physical contact, added dialogue, invented murmuring, music, subtitles or added story beat.

Avoid: Jitter, bent or distorted limbs. Temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

Prompt text

Download exact text

[PROMPT START]
Continue from @video1.

[TITLE]
Absolutely Not Potter

[REFERENCES]
@video1: Video continuation reference for character state, environment, spatial continuity, visual style and established voices.
@image1: Malfoid's identity and uniform; exclude the static full-body wand-display pose and pale backdrop.
@image2: Male 1's identity, uniform and cheek scrape; exclude the expression-panel collage and turnaround lineup.
@image3: Foideldore's hood-up back silhouette and cloak only; exclude every front/profile view and all readable feminine features.
@image4: Office architecture, furnishings and materials; do not reproduce the empty symmetrical composition.
@image5: Start frame.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive character acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Malfoid’s annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes, an affectionate smile and pink cheeks. Warm pink haze and small rose-pink hearts accompany the daydream. A rapid headshake and sharp snap-zoom out restore the office and her defensive annoyance before the exaggerated spoken outburst.

[DURATION]
30 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud posture and precise diction; her eyes and shoulders betray feelings before she can recover her pose.
State in this scene: Standing right of and slightly behind Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded, watching him rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome brings a complete change into dreamy affection and a visible blush.
Motion anchor: Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile; she blushes and raises her hands beneath her chin. After the affectionate thought, a rapid side-to-side headshake breaks the daydream. She crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1 during her internal refusal, then unfolds her arms to point at him and shouts with exaggerated indignation, still blushing. Facial acting carries the thoughts; visible speech articulation begins with her spoken confrontation.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register and precise articulation. The handsome thought is soft, dreamy and sincerely affectionate internal voiceover. The internal refusal becomes brisk and emphatic after the headshake. Her first spoken interruption is a sharp, exaggerated shout with biting emphasis on his famous name; later accusations remain dry and pointed. Voice identity remains consistent through these changes.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Standing on the visitor side of the desk, left of and slightly forward of Malfoid, finishing an appeal toward Foideldore at the windows.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. Never flirts deliberately, touches her or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Standing on the window side of the desk, facing the windows with her back toward Male 1 and Malfoid, silent.
Motion anchor: No turn or visible face, hair, profile or gender cue. Frustration accumulates through stiffening shoulders and a small head drop.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Layout: The desk stands between the visitor area and the three pointed windows.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–06.5 seconds:
Starting from @image5, cut to a three-quarter frontal medium close-up of Malfoid watching Male 1. The camera slowly pushes toward her face. Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile. Her cheeks blush as she raises her hands beneath her chin and thinks to herself in a dreamy, affectionate inner voice, 「嘘……かっこいい。」 Simultaneously, the office background gradually blurs into a warm pink haze filled with small, translucent rose-pink hearts, softly glowing and drifting upward behind her. The push-in ends on her lovestruck face as the thought finishes.

She suddenly shakes her head rapidly from side to side. As she finishes the shake, the camera snap-zooms out to a medium shot and the romantic background instantly returns to the clearly defined office, restoring its amber light and blue window rim. Her expression immediately sharpens back into an annoyed frown, her cheeks still flushed. She crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1, thinking to herself in an emphatic, self-rebuking inner voice, 「ダメ。絶対にダメ。こいつはポッターよ。」

06.5–13.0 seconds:
Cut to a locked wide side-profile two-shot from the visitor side, the desk behind both students. From the visitor side, Male 1 stands left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind; Foideldore stands beyond the desk near the windows. Both students occupy the foreground in front of the desk's near edge. The desk spans the midground behind both students. Foideldore stands alone in the background, facing the windows with her back toward the students. Malfoid holds her annoyed frown and crossed-arm pose, her cheeks flushed. From her position, she sharply turns toward Male 1, unfolds her arms and throws one hand out to point at him, leaning into the accusation with an exaggeratedly indignant expression and flushed cheeks. She shouts, {誰にも庇ってもらう必要なんてないわ。特に、かの有名なハリー・ポッターにはね。} The first sentence bursts out; his famous name receives pointed, biting emphasis. Warm light holds the students; a distant blue rim outlines Foideldore’s hood. <Robe and sleeve movement.>

13.0–17.5 — SHOT 10:
Camera: Locked medium over Malfoid's white-haired shoulder toward Male 1.
Action: Malfoid lowers her pointing hand. Male 1 turns fully toward Malfoid, brows lifting in genuine confusion.
Lighting: Amber face; blue edge on black hair and glasses.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君の代わりに戦ってるんじゃない。何があったか話してるだけだ。」
Delivery: Baffled correction, growing friction; factual rather than patronizing.

17.5–20.0 — SHOT 11:
Camera: Locked tight side-profile two-shot, Male 1 left and Malfoid right.
Action: Malfoid leans closer, cheeks flushed and eyes narrowing. Male 1 holds Malfoid’s gaze, forehead creasing while waiting for an explanation.
Lighting: Thin blue rims against the amber room.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「あなたって、いつもそう。」
Delivery: Accusatory familiarity, as though this offence is beyond obvious.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「何が？」
Delivery: Immediate, honestly bewildered.

20.0–23.0 — SHOT 12:
Camera: Locked rear medium close-up on Foideldore's hood and shoulders.
Action: Foideldore’s shoulders become markedly rigid. The hood tips down a fraction while Malfoid continues accusing Male 1 offscreen. Foideldore remains completely back-facing; the heavy robe hides her body.
Lighting: Nearly black hood silhouette with a hard blue rim.
Dialogue: MALFOID, offscreen; 「何でも自分のことみたいに首を突っ込む。」
Delivery: Sharp and increasingly personal; not an inner thought.
SFX: Window wind beneath her voice.

23.0–26.5 — SHOT 13:
Camera: Locked tight three-quarter frontal close-up on Male 1 from Malfoid's side.
Action: Male 1 looks directly at Malfoid as his frustrated brow releases and his shoulders settle. Holding Malfoid’s gaze, Male 1 states the simplest possible truth.
Lighting: Blue on one cheek; amber holds the other.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君に命を救われたんだ。だったら僕の問題だ。」
Delivery: Plain, firm and emotionally exposed; give the second sentence its own weight.

26.5–30.0 — SHOT 14:
Camera: Tight frontal close-up on Malfoid from Male 1's eyeline; slow push inward.
Action: Malfoid holds Male 1’s gaze for a beat too long, her flushed cheeks turning a deeper red. Her annoyed frown tightens as her eyes linger on his face. Her eyes snap aside before her head follows. She turns partly away, folds her arms and delivers her complaint with pointed irritation, her cheeks still red.
Lighting: Warm face through the hold; stronger blue hair rim as she turns.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「もう……ほんっと、イライラする。」
Delivery: Flustered exasperation with pointed stress on the complaint; frustration at her own feelings keeps her tone irritated.
VFX: Held, flushed and visibly irritated eye contact followed by a sharply accented gaze break.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Exact Japanese lines, no additions or paraphrases. Inner voice is dry and close, without lip sync; spoken dialogue uses natural office acoustics. No invented murmuring, music or subtitles. Cuts follow complete lines.
Subtext: Malfoid watches Male 1 rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome draws her into genuine, dreamy affection and makes her blush. She interrupts that state with a rapid headshake, thinks her refusal, recovers her defensive annoyance and snaps at him. Male 1 means his gratitude literally. Foideldore’s motionless back accumulates pressure while their argument grows more volatile.
Critical constraint: @image5 is the start frame. Preserve the chronological action, all exact Japanese wording and the 30-second duration. The first range combines source beats 07–08; the outburst occupies 06.5–13.0, and all later boundaries are retained. Both thought lines remain internal voiceover, with no lip synchronization to their words. The headshake follows the affectionate thought, the snap-zoom out follows the headshake, and the private refusal precedes the spoken snap. Foideldore remains back-facing and concealed throughout: no gender, face, profile, hair, body-contour or reflection reveal. Preserve reference identities without display poses. Both students remain on the visitor side of the desk; Foideldore remains on the opposite window side. From the visitor-side view, Male 1 is left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind. Preserve these physical positions through angle changes. Carry the resulting character state through subsequent cuts until a described action changes it. No drawn wands, deliberate flirting by Male 1, kiss, physical contact, added dialogue, invented murmuring, music, subtitles or added story beat.
Animation style: Full-range Japanese anime acting: eye darts, blush, sharp chin lifts, leaning silhouettes and delayed hair/sleeve follow-through. Strong poses and held reactions, not continuous low-intensity microexpression acting.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

Warm light holds the students; a distant blue rim outlines Foideldore’s hood. <Robe and sleeve movement.>

13.0–17.5 — SHOT 10:
Camera: Locked medium over Malfoid's white-haired shoulder toward Male 1.
Action: Malfoid lowers her pointing hand. Male 1 turns fully toward Malfoid, brows lifting in genuine confusion.
Lighting: Amber face; blue edge on black hair and glasses.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君の代わりに戦ってるんじゃない。何があったか話してるだけだ。」
Delivery: Baffled correction, growing friction; factual rather than patronizing.

17.5–20.0 — SHOT 11:
Camera: Locked tight side-profile two-shot, Male 1 left and Malfoid right.
Action: Malfoid leans closer, cheeks flushed and eyes narrowing. Male 1 holds Malfoid’s gaze, forehead creasing while waiting for an explanation.
Lighting: Thin blue rims against the amber room.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「あなたって、いつもそう。」
Delivery: Accusatory familiarity, as though this offence is beyond obvious.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「何が？」
Delivery: Immediate, honestly bewildered.

20.0–23.0 — SHOT 12:
Camera: Locked rear medium close-up on Foideldore's hood and shoulders.
Action: Foideldore’s shoulders become markedly rigid. The hood tips down a fraction while Malfoid continues accusing Male 1 offscreen. Foideldore remains completely back-facing; the heavy robe hides her body.
Lighting: Nearly black hood silhouette with a hard blue rim.
Dialogue: MALFOID, offscreen; 「何でも自分のことみたいに首を突っ込む。」
Delivery: Sharp and increasingly personal; not an inner thought.
SFX: Window wind beneath her voice.

23.0–26.5 — SHOT 13:
Camera: Locked tight three-quarter frontal close-up on Male 1 from Malfoid's side.
Action: Male 1 looks directly at Malfoid as his frustrated brow releases and his shoulders settle. Holding Malfoid’s gaze, Male 1 states the simplest possible truth.
Lighting: Blue on one cheek; amber holds the other.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君に命を救われたんだ。だったら僕の問題だ。」
Delivery: Plain, firm and emotionally exposed; give the second sentence its own weight.

26.5–30.0 — SHOT 14:
Camera: Tight frontal close-up on Malfoid from Male 1's eyeline; slow push inward.
Action: Malfoid holds Male 1’s gaze for a beat too long, her flushed cheeks turning a deeper red. Her annoyed frown tightens as her eyes linger on his face. Her eyes snap aside before her head follows. She turns partly away, folds her arms and delivers her complaint with pointed irritation, her cheeks still red.
Lighting: Warm face through the hold; stronger blue hair rim as she turns.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「もう……ほんっと、イライラする。」
Delivery: Flustered exasperation with pointed stress on the complaint; frustration at her own feelings keeps her tone irritated.
VFX: Held, flushed and visibly irritated eye contact followed by a sharply accented gaze break.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Exact Japanese lines, no additions or paraphrases. Inner voice is dry and close, without lip sync; spoken dialogue uses natural office acoustics. No invented murmuring, music or subtitles. Cuts follow complete lines.
Subtext: Malfoid watches Male 1 rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome draws her into genuine, dreamy affection and makes her blush. She interrupts that state with a rapid headshake, thinks her refusal, recovers her defensive annoyance and snaps at him. Male 1 means his gratitude literally. Foideldore’s motionless back accumulates pressure while their argument grows more volatile.
Critical constraint: @image5 is the start frame. Preserve the chronological action, all exact Japanese wording and the 30-second duration. The first range combines source beats 07–08; the outburst occupies 06.5–13.0, and all later boundaries are retained. Both thought lines remain internal voiceover, with no lip synchronization to their words. The headshake follows the affectionate thought, the snap-zoom out follows the headshake, and the private refusal precedes the spoken snap. Foideldore remains back-facing and concealed throughout: no gender, face, profile, hair, body-contour or reflection reveal. Preserve reference identities without display poses. Both students remain on the visitor side of the desk; Foideldore remains on the opposite window side. From the visitor-side view, Male 1 is left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind. Preserve these physical positions through angle changes. Carry the resulting character state through subsequent cuts until a described action changes it. No drawn wands, deliberate flirting by Male 1, kiss, physical contact, added dialogue, invented murmuring, music, subtitles or added story beat.
Animation style: Full-range Japanese anime acting: eye darts, blush, sharp chin lifts, leaning silhouettes and delayed hair/sleeve follow-through. Strong poses and held reactions, not continuous low-intensity microexpression acting.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.
