[PROMPT START]
Continue from @video1.

[TITLE]
Absolutely Not Potter

[REFERENCES]
@video1: Video continuation reference for character state, environment, spatial continuity, visual style and established voices.
@image1: Malfoid's identity and uniform; exclude the static full-body wand-display pose and pale backdrop.
@image2: Male 1's identity, uniform and cheek scrape; exclude the expression-panel collage and turnaround lineup.
@image3: Foideldore's hood-up back silhouette and cloak only; exclude every front/profile view and all readable feminine features.
@image4: Office architecture, furnishings and materials; do not reproduce the empty symmetrical composition.
@image5: Start frame.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive character acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Malfoid’s annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes, an affectionate smile and pink cheeks. Warm pink haze and small rose-pink hearts accompany the daydream. A rapid headshake and sharp snap-zoom out restore the office and her defensive annoyance before the exaggerated spoken outburst.

[DURATION]
30 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud posture and precise diction; her eyes and shoulders betray feelings before she can recover her pose.
State in this scene: Standing right of and slightly behind Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded, watching him rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome brings a complete change into dreamy affection and a visible blush.
Motion anchor: Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile; she blushes and raises her hands beneath her chin. After the affectionate thought, a rapid side-to-side headshake breaks the daydream. She crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1 during her internal refusal, then quickly points at Male 1 and shouts with exaggerated indignation, still blushing. Facial acting carries the thoughts; visible speech articulation begins with her spoken confrontation.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register and precise articulation. The handsome thought is soft, dreamy and sincerely affectionate internal voiceover. The internal refusal becomes brisk and emphatic after the headshake. Her first spoken interruption is a sharp, exaggerated shout with biting emphasis on his famous name; later accusations remain dry and pointed. Voice identity remains consistent through these changes.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Standing on the visitor side of the desk, left of and slightly forward of Malfoid, finishing an appeal toward Foideldore at the windows.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. Never flirts deliberately, touches her or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Standing on the window side of the desk, facing the windows with her back toward Male 1 and Malfoid, silent.
Motion anchor: No turn or visible face, hair, profile or gender cue. Frustration accumulates through stiffening shoulders and a small head drop.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Layout: The desk stands between the visitor area and the three pointed windows.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–06.5 seconds:
Starting from @image5, cut to a three-quarter frontal medium close-up of Malfoid watching Male 1. The camera slowly pushes toward her face. Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile. Her cheeks blush as she raises her hands beneath her chin and thinks to herself in a dreamy, affectionate inner voice, 「嘘……かっこいい。」 Simultaneously, the office background gradually blurs into a warm pink haze filled with small, translucent rose-pink hearts, softly glowing and drifting upward behind her. The push-in ends on her lovestruck face as the thought finishes.

She suddenly shakes her head rapidly from side to side. As she finishes the shake, the camera snap-zooms out to a medium shot and the romantic background instantly returns to the clearly defined office, restoring its amber light and blue window rim. Her expression immediately sharpens back into an annoyed frown, her cheeks still flushed. She crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1, thinking to herself in an emphatic, self-rebuking inner voice, 「ダメ。絶対にダメ。こいつはポッターよ。」

06.5–13.0 seconds:
Cut to a locked wide side-profile two-shot of Malfoid and Male 1. Malfoid quickly points at Male 1 and takes a defensive, embarrassed stance, leaning into the accusation. Her cheeks remain flushed from embarrassment at her own thought as she shouts with exaggerated indignation, {誰にも庇ってもらう必要なんてないわ。} The first sentence bursts out, as Male 1 leans back against the desk’s near edge, drawing his face away from Malfoid’s finger with a comically nervous grimace. His eyes are wide and his brows pinched, with dark stress shading across his upper face and sweat beads at his temples. The camera cuts to a close OTS from behind Malfoid, framing Malfoid’s pointing hand in the foreground and Male 1 in close-up as Male 1 continues leaning back to avoid the finger, keeping his nervous grimace while Malfoid continues shouting, {特に、かの有名なハリー・ポッターにはね。}, the name landing with biting emphasis. In the backdrop beyond Male 1, Foideldore remains standing behind the desk, close to the windows, with her back to the camera. <Robe and sleeve movement.>

Lighting: Amber face; blue edge on black hair and glasses.

Delivery: Immediate, honestly bewildered.

Lighting: Nearly black hood silhouette with a hard blue rim.

Delivery: Sharp and increasingly personal; not an inner thought.
SFX: Window wind beneath her voice.

[DURATION]
30 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud posture and precise diction; her eyes and shoulders betray feelings before she can recover her pose.
State in this scene: Standing right of and slightly behind Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded, watching him rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome brings a complete change into dreamy affection and a visible blush.
Motion anchor: Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile; she blushes and raises her hands beneath her chin. After the affectionate thought, a rapid side-to-side headshake breaks the daydream. She crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1 during her internal refusal, then quickly points at Male 1 and shouts with exaggerated indignation, still blushing. Facial acting carries the thoughts; visible speech articulation begins with her spoken confrontation.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register and precise articulation. The handsome thought is soft, dreamy and sincerely affectionate internal voiceover. The internal refusal becomes brisk and emphatic after the headshake. Her first spoken interruption is a sharp, exaggerated shout with biting emphasis on his famous name; later accusations remain dry and pointed. Voice identity remains consistent through these changes.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Standing on the visitor side of the desk, left of and slightly forward of Malfoid, finishing an appeal toward Foideldore at the windows.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. Never flirts deliberately, touches her or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Standing on the window side of the desk, facing the windows with her back toward Male 1 and Malfoid, silent.
Motion anchor: No turn or visible face, hair, profile or gender cue. Frustration accumulates through stiffening shoulders and a small head drop.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Layout: The desk stands between the visitor area and the three pointed windows.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–06.5 seconds:
Starting from @image5, cut to a three-quarter frontal medium close-up of Malfoid watching Male 1. The camera slowly pushes toward her face. Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile. Her cheeks blush as she raises her hands beneath her chin and thinks to herself in a dreamy, affectionate inner voice, 「嘘……かっこいい。」 Simultaneously, the office background gradually blurs into a warm pink haze filled with small, translucent rose-pink hearts, softly glowing and drifting upward behind her. The push-in ends on her lovestruck face as the thought finishes.

She suddenly shakes her head rapidly from side to side. As she finishes the shake, the camera snap-zooms out to a medium shot and the romantic background instantly returns to the clearly defined office, restoring its amber light and blue window rim. Her expression immediately sharpens back into an annoyed frown, her cheeks still flushed. She crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1, thinking to herself in an emphatic, self-rebuking inner voice, 「ダメ。絶対にダメ。こいつはポッターよ。」

06.5–13.0 seconds:
Cut to a locked wide side-profile two-shot of Malfoid and Male 1. Malfoid quickly points at Male 1 and takes a defensive, embarrassed stance, leaning into the accusation. Her cheeks remain flushed from embarrassment at her own thought as she shouts with exaggerated indignation, {誰にも庇ってもらう必要なんてないわ。} The first sentence bursts out, as Male 1 leans back against the desk’s near edge, drawing his face away from Malfoid’s finger with a comically nervous grimace. His eyes are wide and his brows pinched, with dark stress shading across his upper face and sweat beads at his temples. The camera cuts to a close OTS from behind Malfoid, framing Malfoid’s pointing hand in the foreground and Male 1 in close-up as Male 1 continues leaning back to avoid the finger, keeping his nervous grimace while Malfoid continues shouting, {特に、かの有名なハリー・ポッターにはね。}, the name landing with biting emphasis. In the backdrop beyond Male 1, Foideldore remains standing behind the desk, close to the windows, with her back to the camera. <Robe and sleeve movement.>

13.0–17.5 — SHOT 10:
Camera: Locked medium over Malfoid's white-haired shoulder toward Male 1.
Action: Malfoid lowers her pointing hand while Male 1 replies from the leaned-back position against the desk’s near edge, with the nervous grimace, dark stress shading and sweat beads still visible.
Lighting: Amber face; blue edge on black hair and glasses.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君の代わりに戦ってるんじゃない。何があったか話してるだけだ。」
Delivery: Baffled correction, growing friction; factual rather than patronizing.

17.5–20.0 — SHOT 11:
Camera: Locked tight side-profile two-shot, Male 1 left and Malfoid right.
Action: Malfoid leans closer, cheeks flushed and eyes narrowing. Male 1 holds Malfoid’s gaze, forehead creasing while waiting for an explanation.
Lighting: Thin blue rims against the amber room.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「あなたって、いつもそう。」
Delivery: Accusatory familiarity, as though this offence is beyond obvious.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「何が？」
Delivery: Immediate, honestly bewildered.

20.0–23.0 — SHOT 12:
Camera: Locked rear medium close-up on Foideldore's hood and shoulders.
Action: Foideldore’s shoulders become markedly rigid. The hood tips down a fraction while Malfoid continues accusing Male 1 offscreen. Foideldore remains completely back-facing; the heavy robe hides her body.
Lighting: Nearly black hood silhouette with a hard blue rim.
Dialogue: MALFOID, offscreen; 「何でも自分のことみたいに首を突っ込む。」
Delivery: Sharp and increasingly personal; not an inner thought.
SFX: Window wind beneath her voice.

23.0–26.5 — SHOT 13:
Camera: Locked tight three-quarter frontal close-up on Male 1 from Malfoid's side.
Action: Male 1 looks directly at Malfoid as his frustrated brow releases and his shoulders settle. Holding Malfoid’s gaze, Male 1 states the simplest possible truth.
Lighting: Blue on one cheek; amber holds the other.
Dialogue: MALE 1; 「君に命を救われたんだ。だったら僕の問題だ。」
Delivery: Plain, firm and emotionally exposed; give the second sentence its own weight.

26.5–30.0 — SHOT 14:
Camera: Tight frontal close-up on Malfoid from Male 1's eyeline; slow push inward.
Action: Malfoid holds Male 1’s gaze for a beat too long, her flushed cheeks turning a deeper red. Her annoyed frown tightens as her eyes linger on his face. Her eyes snap aside before her head follows. She turns partly away, folds her arms and delivers her complaint with pointed irritation, her cheeks still red.
Lighting: Warm face through the hold; stronger blue hair rim as she turns.
Dialogue: MALFOID; 「もう……ほんっと、イライラする。」
Delivery: Flustered exasperation with pointed stress on the complaint; frustration at her own feelings keeps her tone irritated.
VFX: Held, flushed and visibly irritated eye contact followed by a sharply accented gaze break.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Exact Japanese lines, no additions or paraphrases. Inner voice is dry and close, without lip sync; spoken dialogue uses natural office acoustics. No invented murmuring, music or subtitles. Cuts follow complete lines.
Subtext: Malfoid watches Male 1 rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome draws her into genuine, dreamy affection and makes her blush. She interrupts that state with a rapid headshake, thinks her refusal, recovers her defensive annoyance and snaps at him. Male 1 means his gratitude literally. Foideldore’s motionless back accumulates pressure while their argument grows more volatile.
Critical constraint: @image5 is the start frame. Preserve the chronological action, all exact Japanese wording and the 30-second duration. The first range combines source beats 07–08; the outburst occupies 06.5–13.0, and all later boundaries are retained. Both thought lines remain internal voiceover, with no lip synchronization to their words. The headshake follows the affectionate thought, the snap-zoom out follows the headshake, and the private refusal precedes the spoken snap. Foideldore remains back-facing and concealed throughout: no gender, face, profile, hair, body-contour or reflection reveal. Preserve reference identities without display poses. Both students remain on the visitor side of the desk; Foideldore remains on the opposite window side. From the visitor-side view, Male 1 is left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind. Preserve these physical positions through angle changes. Carry the resulting character state through subsequent cuts until a described action changes it. No drawn wands, deliberate flirting by Male 1, kiss, physical contact between characters, added dialogue, invented murmuring, music, subtitles or added story beat.
Animation style: Full-range Japanese anime acting: eye darts, blush, sharp chin lifts, leaning silhouettes and delayed hair/sleeve follow-through. Strong poses and held reactions, not continuous low-intensity microexpression acting.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

[TITLE]
Absolutely Not Potter

[REFERENCES]
@video1: Video continuation reference for character state, environment, spatial continuity, visual style and established voices.

@image3: Foideldore's hood-up back silhouette and cloak only; exclude every front/profile view and all readable feminine features.
@image4: Office architecture, furnishings and materials; do not reproduce the empty symmetrical composition.
@image5: Start frame.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive character acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Malfoid’s annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes, an affectionate smile and pink cheeks. Warm pink haze and small rose-pink hearts accompany the daydream. A rapid headshake and sharp snap-zoom out restore the office and her defensive annoyance before the exaggerated spoken outburst.

[DURATION]
30 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud posture and precise diction; her eyes and shoulders betray feelings before she can recover her pose.
State in this scene: Standing right of and slightly behind Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded, watching him rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome brings a complete change into dreamy affection and a visible blush.
Motion anchor: Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile; she blushes and raises her hands beneath her chin. After the affectionate thought, she breaks the daydream with a rapid side-to-side headshake as she starts her internal refusal, crossing her arms and side-eyeing Male 1 while continuing the thought, then quickly points at Male 1 and shouts with exaggerated indignation, still blushing. Facial acting carries the thoughts; visible speech articulation begins with her spoken confrontation.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register and precise articulation. The handsome thought is soft, dreamy and sincerely affectionate internal voiceover. The internal refusal starts briskly and emphatically with the headshake and continues through the side-eye. Her first spoken interruption is a sharp, exaggerated shout with biting emphasis on his famous name; later accusations remain dry and pointed. Voice identity remains consistent through these changes.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Standing on the visitor side of the desk, left of and slightly forward of Malfoid, finishing an appeal toward Foideldore at the windows.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. Never flirts deliberately, touches her or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Standing on the window side of the desk, facing the windows with her back toward Male 1 and Malfoid, silent.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]

The push-in ends on her lovestruck face as the thought finishes.

As Malfoid suddenly shakes her head rapidly from side to side, the camera snap-zooms out to a medium shot and the romantic background instantly returns to the clearly defined office, restoring its amber light and blue window rim, while she starts thinking to herself in an emphatic, self-rebuking inner voice, 「ダメ。絶対にダメ。こいつはポッターよ。」 Malfoid crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1 as she continues the same thought, her expression immediately sharpening back into an annoyed frown and her cheeks still flushed.

[PROMPT START]
Continue from @video1.

[TITLE]
The Limit of Her Protection

[REFERENCES]
@video1: Video continuation reference for character state, environment, spatial continuity, visual style and established voices.
@image1: Malfoid's identity and uniform; exclude the full-body wand-display stance and pale background.
@image2: Male 1's identity, uniform and cheek scrape; exclude the expression panels and turnaround lineup.
@image3: Foideldore's hood-up design. Before the Shot 16 turn, use only the concealing back silhouette. Her front/profile design becomes available only as she turns. Never reproduce the three-view sheet.
@image4: Office geography, furnishings and materials; do not copy the empty symmetrical view instead of the specified shots.
@image5: Selected original-source continuation frame for character state, spatial continuity, lighting and visual style. The shot sequence defines the camera angles.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, graphic silhouettes, expressive acting; impact frame only at the desk slam.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Compressed anger breaks violently into authority, punctures the students' denial, then gives way to a warning carrying genuine uncertainty.

[DURATION]
30 seconds. Shot 16 is exactly 6.0 seconds, one uninterrupted shot.

[CHARACTERS]
FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3; revealed as a mature woman only during the Shot 16 turn.
Features: Heavy wine-red floor-length cloak, broad sleeves, navy-lined raised hood. After the turn: silver-gray waves beneath the hood, round glasses, dark charcoal inner top, purple sash, long dark skirt and black boots, matching the reference.
State in this scene: Standing at the windows, back toward the desk, frustration accumulated; one hand hangs beside her robes.
Personality: Stubborn stillness gives way to a forceful physical eruption. Regaining control takes a visible breath and deliberate release of muscular tension.
Motion anchor: Shot 15 closes the fist; Shot 16 starts with it already clenched. Gender, face, hair and body contours remain concealed until the turn. Hood stays raised; the turn reveals the face within it. No premature reflection or costume insert.
Voice: Mature Japanese female, slightly low and warm, subtly husky, conversational authority and dry humor. Full-force command in Shot 16; angry rebuke, then grounded concern. Never aristocratic or ceremonially polished.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loosened burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Visitor side of the desk, left of Malfoid, turned toward her after their argument.
Motion anchor: Stops at the slam, glances toward Malfoid after Foideldore's rebuke, then gives the Ministry warning his full attention. No spoken line or drawn wand.

MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
State in this scene: Right of Male 1, arms refolded, head partly averted after a flustered complaint.
Motion anchor: Arms fall at the interruption. Catches his glance, abruptly faces forward and refuses to look back. At the Ministry warning, shoulders tighten while her face regains composure. No spoken line or drawn wand.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's stone tower room, dark wooden floor, heavy carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; candles, stacked books, parchment and brass instruments. On the desk: books, parchment, ink bottles and small brass objects, all maintaining positions until the slam disturbs them.
Blocking: Male 1 left and Malfoid right on the visitor side. Foideldore stands beyond the desk at the windows, then approaches its far edge. No one changes sides.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–01.5 — SHOT 15:
Camera: Locked extreme close-up on Foideldore's hand hanging beside the robe.
Action: Fingers slowly curl inward until the hand forms a tight fist. Nothing else is visible; sleeve and framing conceal identifying features.
Lighting: Cold rim on hand and sleeve, warm reflected light beneath.
SFX: Quiet office ambience; the preceding exchange falls away without added words.

01.5–07.5 — SHOT 16:
Duration: 6.0s.
Camera: Slight low-angle medium-wide shot from behind Male 1 and Malfoid, looking across the desk toward Foideldore at the tall windows. Male 1 and Malfoid frame the near foreground edges. One continuous slow zoom toward Foideldore.
Action: Foideldore remains facing the windows with her back toward the room, her fist already clenched at her side.
The camera slowly closes in.
Her shoulders tighten further. Her posture becomes rigid. Her head lowers slightly.
As the zoom continues, Male 1 and Malfoid gradually disappear from the edges of frame and Foideldore dominates the composition.
At the peak of the tension, Foideldore suddenly turns toward them.
Because the camera is already positioned slightly low, she immediately becomes imposing as she turns. She strides toward the desk, growing rapidly larger in frame.
Foideldore SLAMS both palms hard onto the desk.
On impact, focus holds on her hands in the near foreground as books, parchment, ink bottles, and small brass objects jump and rattle.
The focus then racks from her planted hands up to her face.
Foideldore glares directly toward Male 1 and Malfoid.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「いい加減にしなさい！」
Delivery: An explosive command, chest-supported and sharply articulated; no pre-turn vocal cue.
SFX: Quick footsteps toward the desk. Heavy desk impact. Books, parchment, ink bottles, and small desk objects jump and rattle.
Lighting: Cold moonlight creates a strong rim around Foideldore at the windows. As she approaches the desk, warm interior light increasingly catches her face. After the impact, her hands are strongly defined in the foreground before the rack focus settles on her face.
VFX: Strong anime impact frame at the instant both hands hit the desk; brief graphic vibration through the desk and nearby objects.

07.5–15.5 — SHOT 17:
Camera: Locked medium over Foideldore's shoulder toward both students across the desk.
Action: She remains leaned over her planted palms, looking between them. Male 1 straightens, argument stopped. Malfoid's arms have dropped; both now look at Foideldore. Her anger stays visible through a set jaw and firm forward weight; the reference's mature female identity is now fully readable.
Lighting: Warm students; blue-edged darker foreground shoulder.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「お互い嫌いなふりは、そのくらいにしなさい。続きは廊下で。今はもっと大事な話があります。」
Delivery: Angry, dry and pointed; punctuate the corridor instruction, then turn the final sentence toward the serious matter. Not a playful wink.
SFX: Desk objects finish settling beneath her speech.

15.5–16.5 — SHOT 18:
Camera: Locked high-angle medium two-shot from Foideldore's side of the desk.
Action: Male 1 glances sideways toward Malfoid. She catches him immediately; eyes widen for an instant, then snap forward. Spine stiffens into aggressively neutral posture. He keeps looking another beat; she refuses to look back.
Lighting: Warm faces and blue window edging.
SFX: Office room tone; no dialogue or comic sound effect.

16.5–21.0 — SHOT 19:
Camera: Rear three-quarter medium beside Foideldore at the desk; slow pull-back.
Action: Pause. She closes her eyes and exhales; fingers release their pressure. Shoulders lower. She straightens, removes both hands from the desk and smooths her robes once. Her eyes reopen after the explosive anger has passed; the students remain beyond her.
Lighting: Warm interior light softens across her face and robes.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「ハリー、その言い分は魔法省に取っておきなさい。」
Delivery: A deliberate reset into practical authority; his name draws his attention without another reprimand.
SFX: Audible exhale, soft fabric, distant window wind.

21.0–22.5 — SHOT 20:
Camera: Locked medium over Foideldore's shoulder toward Male 1 and Malfoid.
Action: Male 1's remaining argumentative tension drops. Hands settle; he becomes fully attentive. Malfoid stays upright, but the mention of the Ministry tightens her shoulders beneath the robe. Her face remains composed. Do not play this as romantic embarrassment.
Lighting: Warm students, darker foreground shoulder.
SFX: Quiet office ambience.

22.5–30.0 — SHOT 21:
Camera: Tight three-quarter frontal medium close-up on Foideldore across the desk; very slow push-in.
Action: She looks toward Male 1, then shifts her gaze directly to Malfoid. No anger remains. She holds Malfoid's offscreen eyeline steadily through the warning and its aftermath; Malfoid holds it back without her earlier combative posture. End on Foideldore, not an added reaction shot.
Lighting: Warm facial planes against the cold blue windows.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「もし魔法省が聞く耳を持たなければ……いずれ、私にも止められなくなるかもしれません。」
Delivery: Grounded concern, a meaningful pause at the ellipsis; admits the limit of her protection rather than threatening Malfoid. Leave a held beat after the complete line.
SFX: Distant wind against the glass. Cut.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Exact Japanese dialogue only; Foideldore is the sole speaker. No new student lines or unintelligible ad-libs. Physical footsteps, desk impact, rattling objects, fabric and wind. No music, tonal drone or subtitles in this baseline.
Subtext: Foideldore's explosive authority ends their defensive romantic argument. Once control returns, the danger is institutional and her ability to protect them is finite.
Critical constraint: All seven shots remain in order. Shot 16 retains its exact six-second continuous slow zoom, back-facing tension, sudden turn, approach, two-palm slam, hands-focus and rack to face. No cut, snap zoom, dolly substitute, speed ramp or early face insert. Gender becomes visually readable only during the turn. No hood removal. Reference display compositions never replace staging. Cut outside complete lines; no new narrative event.
Animation style: Deliberately rigid anticipation, sudden whole-body release, weighted robe follow-through, one impact frame on contact. Strong student recoil and embarrassed recovery, then a distinct shift to the sober warning.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, repeated desk impacts, extra fist-clenching in Shot 16, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

Features: Long platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
State in this scene: Right of Male 1, arms refolded, head partly averted after a flustered complaint.
Motion anchor: Arms fall at the interruption. Catches his glance, abruptly faces forward and refuses to look back. At the Ministry warning, shoulders tighten while her face regains composure. No spoken line or drawn wand.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's stone tower room, dark wooden floor, heavy carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; candles, stacked books, parchment and brass instruments. On the desk: books, parchment, ink bottles and small brass objects, all maintaining positions until the slam disturbs them.
Blocking: Male 1 left and Malfoid right on the visitor side. Foideldore stands beyond the desk at the windows, then approaches its far edge. No one changes sides.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–01.5 — SHOT 15:
Camera: Locked extreme close-up on Foideldore's hand hanging beside the robe.
Action: Fingers slowly curl inward until the hand forms a tight fist. Nothing else is visible; sleeve and framing conceal identifying features.
Lighting: Cold rim on hand and sleeve, warm reflected light beneath.
SFX: Quiet office ambience; the preceding exchange falls away without added words.

01.5–07.5 — SHOT 16:
Duration: 6.0s.
Camera: Slight low-angle medium-wide shot from behind Male 1 and Malfoid, looking across the desk toward Foideldore at the tall windows. Male 1 and Malfoid frame the near foreground edges. One continuous slow zoom toward Foideldore.
Action: Foideldore remains facing the windows with her back toward the room, her fist already clenched at her side.
The camera slowly closes in.
Her shoulders tighten further. Her posture becomes rigid. Her head lowers slightly.
As the zoom continues, Male 1 and Malfoid gradually disappear from the edges of frame and Foideldore dominates the composition.
At the peak of the tension, Foideldore suddenly turns toward them.
Because the camera is already positioned slightly low, she immediately becomes imposing as she turns. She strides toward the desk, growing rapidly larger in frame.
Foideldore SLAMS both palms hard onto the desk.
On impact, focus holds on her hands in the near foreground as books, parchment, ink bottles, and small brass objects jump and rattle.
The focus then racks from her planted hands up to her face.
Foideldore glares directly toward Male 1 and Malfoid.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「いい加減にしなさい！」
Delivery: An explosive command, chest-supported and sharply articulated; no pre-turn vocal cue.
SFX: Quick footsteps toward the desk. Heavy desk impact. Books, parchment, ink bottles, and small desk objects jump and rattle.
Lighting: Cold moonlight creates a strong rim around Foideldore at the windows. As she approaches the desk, warm interior light increasingly catches her face. After the impact, her hands are strongly defined in the foreground before the rack focus settles on her face.
VFX: Strong anime impact frame at the instant both hands hit the desk; brief graphic vibration through the desk and nearby objects.

07.5–15.5 — SHOT 17:
Camera: Locked medium over Foideldore's shoulder toward both students across the desk.
Action: She remains leaned over her planted palms, looking between them. Male 1 straightens, argument stopped. Malfoid's arms have dropped; both now look at Foideldore. Her anger stays visible through a set jaw and firm forward weight; the reference's mature female identity is now fully readable.
Lighting: Warm students; blue-edged darker foreground shoulder.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「お互い嫌いなふりは、そのくらいにしなさい。続きは廊下で。今はもっと大事な話があります。」
Delivery: Angry, dry and pointed; punctuate the corridor instruction, then turn the final sentence toward the serious matter. Not a playful wink.
SFX: Desk objects finish settling beneath her speech.

15.5–16.5 — SHOT 18:
Camera: Locked high-angle medium two-shot from Foideldore's side of the desk.
Action: Male 1 glances sideways toward Malfoid. She catches him immediately; eyes widen for an instant, then snap forward. Spine stiffens into aggressively neutral posture. He keeps looking another beat; she refuses to look back.
Lighting: Warm faces and blue window edging.
SFX: Office room tone; no dialogue or comic sound effect.

16.5–21.0 — SHOT 19:
Camera: Rear three-quarter medium beside Foideldore at the desk; slow pull-back.
Action: Pause. She closes her eyes and exhales; fingers release their pressure. Shoulders lower. She straightens, removes both hands from the desk and smooths her robes once. Her eyes reopen after the explosive anger has passed; the students remain beyond her.
Lighting: Warm interior light softens across her face and robes.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「ハリー、その言い分は魔法省に取っておきなさい。」
Delivery: A deliberate reset into practical authority; his name draws his attention without another reprimand.
SFX: Audible exhale, soft fabric, distant window wind.

21.0–22.5 — SHOT 20:
Camera: Locked medium over Foideldore's shoulder toward Male 1 and Malfoid.
Action: Male 1's remaining argumentative tension drops. Hands settle; he becomes fully attentive. Malfoid stays upright, but the mention of the Ministry tightens her shoulders beneath the robe. Her face remains composed. Do not play this as romantic embarrassment.
Lighting: Warm students, darker foreground shoulder.
SFX: Quiet office ambience.

22.5–30.0 — SHOT 21:
Camera: Tight three-quarter frontal medium close-up on Foideldore across the desk; very slow push-in.
Action: She looks toward Male 1, then shifts her gaze directly to Malfoid. No anger remains. She holds Malfoid's offscreen eyeline steadily through the warning and its aftermath; Malfoid holds it back without her earlier combative posture. End on Foideldore, not an added reaction shot.
Lighting: Warm facial planes against the cold blue windows.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「もし魔法省が聞く耳を持たなければ……いずれ、私にも止められなくなるかもしれません。」
Delivery: Grounded concern, a meaningful pause at the ellipsis; admits the limit of her protection rather than threatening Malfoid. Leave a held beat after the complete line.
SFX: Distant wind against the glass. Cut.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Exact Japanese dialogue only; Foideldore is the sole speaker. No new student lines or unintelligible ad-libs. Physical footsteps, desk impact, rattling objects, fabric and wind. No music, tonal drone or subtitles in this baseline.
Subtext: Foideldore's explosive authority ends their defensive romantic argument. Once control returns, the danger is institutional and her ability to protect them is finite.
Critical constraint: All seven shots remain in order. Shot 16 retains its exact six-second continuous slow zoom, back-facing tension, sudden turn, approach, two-palm slam, hands-focus and rack to face. No cut, snap zoom, dolly substitute, speed ramp or early face insert. Gender becomes visually readable only during the turn. No hood removal. Reference display compositions never replace staging. Cut outside complete lines; no new narrative event.
Animation style: Deliberately rigid anticipation, sudden whole-body release, weighted robe follow-through, one impact frame on contact. Strong student recoil and embarrassed recovery, then a distinct shift to the sober warning.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, repeated desk impacts, extra fist-clenching in Shot 16, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, graphic silhouettes, expressive acting; impact frame only at the desk slam.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Compressed anger breaks violently into authority, punctures the students' denial, then gives way to a warning carrying genuine uncertainty.

[DURATION]
30 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3; revealed as a mature woman only during the Shot 16 turn.
Features: Heavy wine-red floor-length cloak, broad sleeves, navy-lined raised hood. After the turn: silver-gray waves beneath the hood, round glasses, dark charcoal inner top, purple sash, long dark skirt and black boots, matching the reference.
State in this scene: Standing at the windows, back toward the desk, frustration accumulated; one hand hangs beside her robes.
Personality: Stubborn stillness gives way to a forceful physical eruption. Regaining control takes a visible breath and deliberate release of muscular tension.
Motion anchor: Shot 15 closes the fist; Shot 16 starts with it already clenched. Hood stays raised through the turn.
Voice: Mature Japanese female, slightly low and warm, subtly husky, conversational authority and dry humor. Full-force command in Shot 16; angry rebuke, then grounded concern. Never aristocratic or ceremonially polished.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loosened burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Visitor side of the desk, left of Malfoid, turned toward her after their argument.

MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
State in this scene: Beside Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms refolded and head partly averted. Her cheeks retain the flush caused by her private thoughts about his handsome face; she covers her affection with irritation.
Motion anchor: Her arms fall at Foideldore’s forceful interruption.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's stone tower room, dark wooden floor, heavy carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; candles, stacked books, parchment and brass instruments. On the desk: books, parchment, ink bottles and small brass objects, all maintaining positions until the slam disturbs them.
Layout: The desk stands between the visitor area and the tall windows.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–01.5 — SHOT 15:
Camera: Locked extreme close-up on Foideldore's hand hanging beside the robe.
Action: Fingers slowly curl inward until the hand forms a tight fist. Nothing else is visible; sleeve and framing conceal identifying features.
Lighting: Cold rim on hand and sleeve, warm reflected light beneath.
SFX: Quiet office ambience; the preceding exchange falls away.

01.5–07.5 — SHOT 16:
Duration: 6.0s.
Camera: Slight low-angle medium-wide shot from behind Male 1 and Malfoid, looking across the desk toward Foideldore at the tall windows. Male 1 and Malfoid frame the near foreground edges. One continuous slow zoom toward Foideldore.
Action: Foideldore remains facing the windows with her back toward the room, her fist already clenched at her side.
The camera slowly closes in.
Her shoulders tighten further. Her posture becomes rigid. Her head lowers slightly.
As the zoom continues, Male 1 and Malfoid gradually disappear from the edges of frame and Foideldore dominates the composition.
At the peak of the tension, Foideldore suddenly turns toward them.
Because the camera is already positioned slightly low, she immediately becomes imposing as she turns. She strides toward the desk, growing rapidly larger in frame.
Foideldore SLAMS both palms hard onto the desk.
On impact, focus holds on her hands in the near foreground as books, parchment, ink bottles, and small brass objects jump and rattle.
The focus then racks from her planted hands up to her face.
Foideldore glares directly toward Male 1 and Malfoid.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「いい加減にしなさい！」
Delivery: An explosive command, chest-supported and sharply articulated; no pre-turn vocal cue.
SFX: Quick footsteps toward the desk. Heavy desk impact. Books, parchment, ink bottles, and small desk objects jump and rattle.
Lighting: Cold moonlight creates a strong rim around Foideldore at the windows. As she approaches the desk, warm interior light increasingly catches her face. After the impact, her hands are strongly defined in the foreground before the rack focus settles on her face.
VFX: Strong anime impact frame at the instant both hands hit the desk; brief graphic vibration through the desk and nearby objects.

07.5–15.5 — SHOT 17:
Camera: Locked medium over Foideldore's shoulder toward both students across the desk.
Blocking: Foideldore’s shoulder is near foreground on the window side. The tabletop spans the midground. Both students remain together across its full width on the far visitor side.
Action: She remains leaned over her planted palms, looking between them. Male 1 straightens, argument stopped. Malfoid's arms have dropped; both now look at Foideldore. Her anger stays visible through a set jaw and firm forward weight; the reference's mature female identity is now fully readable.
Lighting: Warm students; blue-edged darker foreground shoulder.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「お互い嫌いなふりは、そのくらいにしなさい。続きは廊下で。今はもっと大事な話があります。」
Delivery: Angry, dry and pointed; punctuate the corridor instruction, then turn the final sentence toward the serious matter.
SFX: Desk objects finish settling beneath her speech.

15.5–16.5 — SHOT 18:
Camera: Locked high-angle medium two-shot from Foideldore's side of the desk.
Blocking: Male 1 and Malfoid together on the visitor side.
Action: Male 1 glances sideways toward Malfoid. She shoots him a sharp, irritated side-glance, her cheeks still flushed from the preceding exchange. Her brow furrows in flustered annoyance. She abruptly faces Foideldore again, lifts her chin and sets her shoulders in a proud, defensive posture. Male 1 keeps looking for another beat; she pointedly keeps her attention forward.
Lighting: Warm faces and blue window edging.
SFX: Office room tone.

16.5–21.0 — SHOT 19:
Camera: Rear three-quarter medium beside Foideldore at the desk; slow pull-back.
Blocking: Foideldore in the near foreground, the desk in the midground, Male 1 and Malfoid together on the visitor side in the background.
Action: Pause. She closes her eyes and exhales; fingers release their pressure. Shoulders lower. She straightens, removes both hands from the desk and smooths her robes once. Her eyes reopen after the explosive anger has passed.
Lighting: Warm interior light softens across her face and robes.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「ハリー、その言い分は魔法省に取っておきなさい。」
Delivery: A deliberate reset into practical authority; his name draws his attention without another reprimand.
SFX: Audible exhale, soft fabric, distant window wind.

21.0–22.5 — SHOT 20:
Camera: Locked medium over Foideldore's shoulder toward Male 1 and Malfoid.
Blocking: Foideldore’s shoulder in the near foreground; Male 1 and Malfoid together beyond the desktop.
Action: Male 1's remaining argumentative tension drops. Hands settle; he becomes fully attentive. Malfoid stays upright, but the mention of the Ministry tightens her shoulders beneath the robe. Her face remains composed.
Lighting: Warm students, darker foreground shoulder.
SFX: Quiet office ambience.

22.5–30.0 — SHOT 21:
Camera: Tight three-quarter frontal medium close-up on Foideldore across the desk; very slow push-in.
Blocking: Male 1 and Malfoid are offscreen beside the camera.
Action: She looks toward Male 1, then shifts her gaze directly to Malfoid. No anger remains. She holds Malfoid's offscreen eyeline steadily through the warning and its aftermath; Malfoid holds it back without her earlier combative posture. End on Foideldore.
Lighting: Warm facial planes against the cold blue windows.
Dialogue: FOIDELDORE; 「もし魔法省が聞く耳を持たなければ……いずれ、私にも止められなくなるかもしれません。」
Delivery: Grounded concern, a meaningful pause at the ellipsis; admits the limit of her protection rather than threatening Malfoid. Leave a held beat after the complete line.
SFX: Distant wind against the glass. Cut.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Exact Japanese dialogue only; Foideldore is the sole speaker. Physical footsteps, desk impact, rattling objects, fabric and wind. No music, tonal drone or subtitles.
Subtext: Malfoid’s irritation conceals physical attraction; being called out sharpens her flustered irritation and proud, defensive posture. Foideldore’s explosive authority ends the argument. Once control returns, the danger is institutional and her ability to protect them is finite.
Critical constraint: Shot 16 remains one uninterrupted six-second slow zoom with its written action and focus progression. Foideldore’s face and gender remain concealed until the turn; her hood stays raised. Male 1 and Malfoid stay together on the visitor side of the desk, Male 1 left and Malfoid right; Foideldore stays on the opposite window side. The students’ wands remain undrawn. Cut outside complete lines.
Animation style: Deliberately rigid anticipation, sudden whole-body release, weighted robe follow-through, one impact frame on contact. Strong student recoil and embarrassed recovery, then a distinct shift to the sober warning.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, repeated desk impacts, extra fist-clenching in Shot 16, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

[PROMPT START]
Continue from @video1.

[TITLE]
A Daydream Interrupted

[REFERENCES]
@video1: Video continuation reference for character state, environment, spatial continuity, visual style and established voices.
@image1: Malfoid's identity and uniform; exclude the static full-body wand-display pose and pale backdrop.
@image2: Male 1's identity, uniform and cheek scrape; exclude the expression-panel collage and turnaround lineup.
@image3: Foideldore's hood-up back silhouette and cloak only; exclude every front/profile view and all readable feminine features.
@image4: Office architecture, furnishings and materials; do not reproduce the empty symmetrical composition.
@image5: Start frame.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive character acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Malfoid’s annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes, an affectionate smile and pink cheeks. Warm pink haze and small rose-pink hearts accompany the daydream. A rapid headshake and sharp snap-zoom out restore the office and her defensive annoyance.

[DURATION]
7 seconds: the replacement performance occupies 00.0–06.5; the final 0.5 seconds are a trailing edit handle.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
Personality: Proud posture and precise diction; her eyes and shoulders betray feelings before she can recover her pose.
State in this scene: Standing right of and slightly behind Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded, watching Male 1 with irritation.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register and precise articulation. The handsome thought is soft, dreamy and sincerely affectionate internal voiceover.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Standing on the visitor side of the desk, left of and slightly forward of Malfoid, facing Foideldore at the windows.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Standing on the window side of the desk, facing the windows with her back toward Male 1 and Malfoid, silent.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Layout: The desk stands between the visitor area and the three pointed windows.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–06.5 seconds:
Starting from @image5, cut to a three-quarter frontal medium close-up of Malfoid watching Male 1. The camera slowly pushes toward her face. Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile. Her cheeks blush as she raises her hands beneath her chin and thinks to herself in a dreamy, affectionate inner voice, 「嘘……かっこいい。」 Simultaneously, the office background gradually blurs into a warm pink haze filled with small, translucent rose-pink hearts, softly glowing and drifting upward behind her. The push-in ends on her lovestruck face as the thought finishes.

As Malfoid suddenly shakes her head rapidly from side to side, the camera snap-zooms out to a medium shot and the romantic background instantly returns to the clearly defined office, restoring its amber light and blue window rim. Malfoid crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1, her expression immediately sharpening back into an annoyed frown and her cheeks still flushed.

06.5–07.0 — CONTINUING EDIT HANDLE:
Camera: Hold the existing medium framing; the same shot continues.
Action: Malfoid holds the resulting crossed-arm pose and annoyed side-eye toward Male 1, her cheeks still flushed.
Lighting: Unchanged amber office light and blue window rim.
SFX: Quiet office ambience.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Only the quoted Japanese thought is vocalized, as dry, close internal voiceover without lip sync. Quiet office ambience and robe movement. No invented murmuring, music or subtitles.
Subtext: Malfoid’s physical attraction embarrasses her, and her proud defensiveness returns through the headshake and annoyed side-eye.
Critical constraint: Male 1 and Malfoid remain on the visitor side of the desk, Male 1 left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind Male 1. Foideldore remains back-facing on the opposite window side.
Animation style: Full-range Japanese anime acting: eye darts, blush, sharp chin lifts, leaning silhouettes and delayed hair/sleeve follow-through. Strong poses and held reactions, not continuous low-intensity microexpression acting.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

As Malfoid suddenly shakes her head rapidly from side to side, the camera snap-zooms out to a medium shot and the romantic background instantly returns to the clearly defined office, restoring its amber light and blue window rim. Malfoid crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1, her expression immediately sharpening back into an annoyed frown and her cheeks still flushed.

06.5–07.0 — CONTINUING EDIT HANDLE:
Camera: Hold the existing medium framing; the same shot continues.
Action: Malfoid holds the resulting crossed-arm pose and annoyed side-eye toward Male 1, her cheeks still flushed.
Lighting: Unchanged amber office light and blue window rim.
SFX: Quiet office ambience.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Only the quoted Japanese thought is vocalized, as dry, close internal voiceover without lip sync. Quiet office ambience and robe movement. No invented murmuring, music or subtitles.
Subtext: Malfoid’s physical attraction embarrasses her, and her proud defensiveness returns through the headshake and annoyed side-eye.
Critical constraint: Male 1 and Malfoid remain on the visitor side of the desk, Male 1 left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind Male 1. Foideldore remains back-facing on the opposite window side.
Animation style: Full-range Japanese anime acting: eye darts, blush, sharp chin lifts, leaning silhouettes and delayed hair/sleeve follow-through. Strong poses and held reactions, not continuous low-intensity microexpression acting.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

[DURATION]
6.5 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.

State in this scene: Standing right of and slightly behind Male 1 on the visitor side of the desk, arms folded, watching him rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome brings a complete change into dreamy affection and a visible blush.
Motion anchor: Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile; she blushes and raises her hands beneath her chin. After the affectionate thought, she breaks the daydream with a rapid side-to-side headshake, crossing her arms and side-eyeing Male 1, still blushing. Facial acting carries the thought.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register and precise articulation. The handsome thought is soft, dreamy and sincerely affectionate internal voiceover. Voice identity remains consistent through these changes.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Standing on the visitor side of the desk, left of and slightly forward of Malfoid, finishing an appeal toward Foideldore at the windows.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. Never flirts deliberately, touches her or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Standing on the window side of the desk, facing the windows with her back toward Male 1 and Malfoid, silent.
Motion anchor: No turn or visible face, hair, profile or gender cue. Frustration accumulates through stiffening shoulders and a small head drop.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Layout: The desk stands between the visitor area and the three pointed windows.

Subtext: Malfoid watches Male 1 rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome draws her into genuine, dreamy affection and makes her blush. She interrupts that state with a rapid headshake, recovering her defensive annoyance and side-eyeing Male 1. Male 1 means his gratitude literally. Foideldore’s motionless back accumulates pressure while their argument grows more volatile.
Critical constraint: @image5 is the start frame. Preserve the chronological action, all exact Japanese wording and the 6.5-second duration. The first range combines source beats 07–08. The first thought remains internal voiceover, with no lip synchronization to its words. Snap-zoom out during the headshake. Foideldore remains back-facing and concealed throughout: no gender, face, profile, hair, body-contour or reflection reveal. Preserve reference identities without display poses. From the visitor-side view, Male 1 is left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind. Preserve these physical positions through angle changes. Carry the resulting character state through subsequent cuts until a described action changes it. No drawn wands, deliberate flirting by Male 1, kiss, physical contact between characters, added dialogue, invented murmuring, music, subtitles or added story beat.

After the affectionate thought, she breaks the daydream with a rapid side-to-side headshake, crossing her arms and side-eyeing Male 1, still blushing. Facial acting carries the thought.
Voice: Young Japanese female, smooth slightly low register and precise articulation. The handsome thought is soft, dreamy and sincerely affectionate internal voiceover. Voice identity remains consistent through these changes.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Standing on the visitor side of the desk, left of and slightly forward of Malfoid, finishing an appeal toward Foideldore at the windows.
Personality: Open-handed moral urgency; baffled by Malfoid's attack, then completely direct when thanking her.
Motion anchor: Turns from Foideldore toward Malfoid when challenged. Never flirts deliberately, touches her or draws a wand.
Voice: Young Japanese male, grounded mid-range, clear and slightly exhaustion-roughened; confused protest becomes unguarded sincerity.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Standing on the window side of the desk, facing the windows with her back toward Male 1 and Malfoid, silent.
Motion anchor: No turn or visible face, hair, profile or gender cue. Frustration accumulates through stiffening shoulders and a small head drop.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Layout: The desk stands between the visitor area and the three pointed windows.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–09.0 seconds:
Starting from @image5, cut to a three-quarter frontal medium close-up of Malfoid watching Male 1. The camera slowly pushes toward her face. Her annoyed frown slowly changes into a lovestruck expression with dreamy eyes and an affectionate smile. Her cheeks blush as she raises her hands beneath her chin and thinks to herself in a dreamy, affectionate inner voice, 「嘘……かっこいい。」 Simultaneously, the office background gradually blurs into a warm pink haze filled with small, translucent rose-pink hearts, softly glowing and drifting upward behind her. The push-in ends on her lovestruck face as the thought finishes.

As Malfoid suddenly shakes her head rapidly from side to side, the camera snap-zooms out to a medium shot and the romantic background instantly returns to the clearly defined office, restoring its amber light and blue window rim. Malfoid crosses her arms and side-eyes Male 1, her expression immediately sharpening back into an annoyed frown and her cheeks still flushed.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Exact Japanese lines, no additions or paraphrases. Inner voice is dry and close, without lip sync. No invented murmuring, music or subtitles.
Subtext: Malfoid watches Male 1 rant with irritation. The intrusive thought that he is handsome draws her into genuine, dreamy affection and makes her blush. She interrupts that state with a rapid headshake, recovering her defensive annoyance and side-eyeing Male 1. Male 1 means his gratitude literally. Foideldore’s motionless back accumulates pressure while their argument grows more volatile.
Critical constraint: @image5 is the start frame. Preserve the chronological action, all exact Japanese wording and the 9-second duration. The first range combines source beats 07–08. The first thought remains internal voiceover, with no lip synchronization to its words. Snap-zoom out during the headshake. Foideldore remains back-facing and concealed throughout: no gender, face, profile, hair, body-contour or reflection reveal. Preserve reference identities without display poses. Both students remain on the visitor side of the desk; Foideldore remains on the opposite window side. From the visitor-side view, Male 1 is left and slightly forward, Malfoid right and slightly behind. Preserve these physical positions through angle changes. Carry the resulting character state through subsequent cuts until a described action changes it. No drawn wands, deliberate flirting by Male 1, kiss, physical contact between characters, added dialogue, invented murmuring, music, subtitles or added story beat.
Animation style: Full-range Japanese anime acting: eye darts, blush, sharp chin lifts, leaning silhouettes and delayed hair/sleeve follow-through. Strong poses and held reactions, not continuous low-intensity microexpression acting.
Avoid: Jitter, distorted limbs, malformed hands, temporal flicker, identity drift, duplicate characters, frozen reference poses, chaotic composition and live-action rendering.

[TITLE]
Annoyed Office Continuation

[REFERENCES]
@video1: Video continuation reference for character state, environment, spatial continuity, visual style and established voices.
@image1: Malfoid's identity and uniform; exclude the static full-body wand-display pose and pale backdrop.
@image2: Male 1's identity, uniform and cheek scrape; exclude the expression-panel collage and turnaround lineup.
@image3: Foideldore's hood-up back silhouette and cloak only; exclude every front/profile view and all readable feminine features.
@image4: Office architecture, furnishings and materials; do not reproduce the empty symmetrical composition.
@image5: Start frame.

[STYLE]
Retro Japanese cel anime, bold linework, Shirow meets Frieren. Mature proportions, painted Gothic interiors, expressive character acting, selective perspective exaggeration.

[VISUAL FEEL]
Defensive annoyance and mounting tension, with clear anime poses, accented shoulder movement and weighted robe follow-through. Warm amber office light and cold blue window edging.

[DURATION]
4 seconds.

[CHARACTERS]
MALFOID:
Base: Young woman matching @image1.
Features: Long straight platinum-white hair, gray-blue eyes; forest-green-lined black robe, cream shirt, green/silver tie, charcoal sweater, dark skirt, opaque tights, black strapped shoes.
State in this scene: Right foreground, arms already crossed, cheeks flushed, narrowed eyes directed frame-left toward Male 1, holding an annoyed frown.
Motion anchor: Keeps her arms crossed throughout the brief shoulder lift and settling movement.

MALE 1:
Base: Young man matching @image2.
Features: Messy blue-black hair, green eyes, black rectangular glasses, cheek scrape; burgundy-lined black robe, ivory shirt, loose burgundy tie, gray-purple sweater, charcoal trousers, brown shoes.
State in this scene: Center frame with his back mostly toward the camera, head directed toward Foideldore at frame-left, arms hanging at his sides.
Motion anchor: Continues addressing Foideldore. His speaking performance reads through a small forward inclination and shoulder emphasis; his head stays directed toward Foideldore.

FOIDELDORE:
Base: Hooded head of school matching @image3's back view.
Features: Broad wine-red floor-length cloak and sleeves, navy-lined raised hood; heavy folds conceal body shape.
State in this scene: Left background at the windows, hood raised and back toward the room, body and arms concealed by the cloak.
Motion anchor: Remains back-facing as tension accumulates through her posture.

[SETTING]
TOWER OFFICE:
Base: @image4's Gothic stone room, dark floorboards, carved desk, curved bookcases and three pointed windows.
Features: Elderly portrait left, lunar chart between windows, Hogwarts landscape right; books, parchment, candles and brass instruments. Cold blue night outside, amber practicals inside.
Layout: The desk stands between the visitor area and the three pointed windows.

[SHOT SEQUENCE]
00.0–04.0 — CONTINUATION:

Action: Starting from @image5, Malfoid keeps her arms crossed and her annoyed side-eye on Male 1 as her shoulders lift briefly with tension and settle, her brows drawing together and her cheeks remaining flushed. At the same time, Male 1 continues addressing Foideldore in the background, inclining his upper body slightly forward with a brief conversational shoulder emphasis. Foideldore’s back becomes more rigid as her hooded head dips slightly toward the windows. These performances overlap within the same continuous shot, with hair and sleeves following the movements.
Lighting: Existing warm amber interior light and cold blue window rim.
SFX: Quiet office ambience and soft robe movement.

[PRODUCTION NOTES]
Audio design: Office room tone and soft cloth movement.
Critical constraint: @image5 is the start frame. Preserve its composition, identities, existing poses and character placement. Foideldore remains hooded and back-facing at the windows. Male 1 keeps addressing Foideldore; Malfoid retains her crossed arms, blush and annoyed side-eye.
